# learner-model decision log

> Why learner-model is built the way it is. Each entry says what was decided, why, what was rejected and where it is described.
>
> **Rule:** do not re-propose what was rejected without new facts. If a decision has to change, ask the author and update this file. An entry is never rewritten: an amendment with its date at the end of the entry corrects it, or a new entry replaces it, and the old one stays where it is, marked "Superseded".
>
> The repository was set up on 2026-10-19 from the template of `spec` v0.1.0 (R21 spec), moved to `spec` v0.2.9 on 2026-10-20 (R01), to v0.6.3 on 2026-11-02 (R02), to v0.6.6 the same day (R05), to v0.9.3 on 2026-11-06 (R11), to v0.9.5 on 2026-11-07 (R15), to v0.9.9 on 2026-11-08 (R17), to v0.9.16 on 2026-11-09 (R19), to v0.12.0 the same day (R23), to v0.14.0 on 2026-11-11 (R26), to v0.15.0 the same day (R27), and to v0.16.3 on 2026-11-13 (R33). How it comes to differ from the template over time — the tools in the devcontainer, the rules in RUN.md — is recorded here as a decision, as R46 canvas-api recorded the differences from its model. Next number — R38.
>
> Here entries are cited as `R03`, in other repositories as `R03 learner-model`. The decisions of `spec` are cited as "R18 spec", MathTrail's decisions as "R37 canvas-api", and in a list as "canvas-api: R37, R38". Each log has its own numbers.
>
> The open questions that concern several repositories are in the registers of `spec`: О- in product 13 spec, П- in privacy 9 spec, Т- in repositories 8 spec. What is still to be decided in the component itself is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.

## The tag of `spec`

**R01. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.2.9: the submodule `spec/` is at this tag, the links to `spec` pinned to a tag name it, and what came from the template is as in the template v0.2.9. Since then the documents are checked by `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo`.**
- **What the tag brings to `learner-model`** (`git diff v0.1.0 v0.2.9` in `spec/`, read 2026-10-20; Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`):
  - R28 spec — the graph types are set by `learner-model`, and `engine` takes them from it; they are still its own and simple, not from `module-kit` (architecture 3.8 spec). The question that T05 would have decided together with `engine` — whether it takes the types from here — is decided; which types they are, T05 decides, checking against T04 engine ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.5);
  - R23 spec — the success corridor is a rule of `engine`: `learner-model` gives the probability of a correct answer, and which corridor to aim at, `engine` decides. This is the answer to the row «в реестр spec» В1 in table 3 of RUN.md, and on the corridor it matched its recommendation; repositories 7 spec and concepts 11 spec were corrected to fit the card. The threshold "mastered", which in standalone is judged by the middle of the corridor and which the recommendation of В1 gave to T01, R23 spec left open: where it lives and how it follows the target of the corridor, T01 and T01 engine decide, and a change of one passes `sim` together with the other. This does not diverge from T01: it decided the threshold and still decides it, now together with `engine`;
  - concepts 12 spec on standalone: when looking for something new, the rule there skipped a mastered topic until all reachable ones were mastered, while a topic returned to the rotation earlier too — when its recommended point rose above the level at which the topic was mastered, or when two errors in a row removed "mastered" from it. The phrase that a mastered topic did not return to the rotation is gone. In concepts 15 spec are the terms "repetition", "rank" and "interface accessibility", and the subitem "Accessibility" in concepts 4 spec became "Opening a skill and access to content";
  - R32 spec — a repository's documents are checked by the `docs-check` of the `spec` its submodule stands on: links, its own sections and log, the tables of questions and of debt, and the citations of `spec` — by the submodule at its tag. An example of a citation is written in inline code so that the check does not take it for a citation (RULES.md, Citations);
  - R30 spec — the CI checks are the same for all repositories, and `learner-model` will have them even before code: secret scanning, DCO, CodeQL, Scorecard and the documents check — the shared checks from `.github`, and with them SonarCloud. All but Scorecard hold the merge into `main`, and only through a pull request, and by the 2026-10-20 amendment to R30 spec also only by a merge commit. T21 `spec` connects them: its session writes the CI files here, together with `dependabot.yml` and the exclusion of `spec/**` for SonarCloud (Д7 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`), and it also sets the ruleset of the branch `main`. The Go checks and with them the shared license check will come with the first code (repositories 6 spec; architecture 3.11 spec);
  - R33 spec — an analyser's finding that a fix cannot close stays open, and its reason is recorded. The reasons shared by all repositories are in R33 spec; a repository's own are written into this log. The new rule 12 of RUN.md has the findings of SonarCloud, CodeQL and Scorecard from the previous task closed before a new one;
  - R31 spec — the release of `spec` comes out by itself, after green CI on `main`: the next patch of the line, immutable, with a GitHub attestation. A repository still moves to a new tag in a task of its own;
  - RULES.md — an example of a citation is written in code (Citations). A development or CI tool that ships nowhere and puts none of its own code into what ships may carry another OSI-approved license too, or free terms for open source, but only as a named exception: in the license check or in the decision that brings it in (Hard rules; R12 spec). This concerns the tools of the future code of `learner-model`;
  - R27 spec — the random generator and the derivation of seeds from `spec` are one for the whole platform: `module-kit`, the widgets, `engine` and `sim`. `learner-model` is not in this list, but its seed too comes from outside (architecture 3.8 spec): if the model needs randomness, the generator and the derivation of seeds are the same ones, not its own ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 1; Т-18);
  - R24–R26 and R29 spec — about other components: the key for the eval, the page of samples, the account with the owner's profile and the children's profiles, the engine's reason codes. They do not change the documents of `learner-model`, nor do the new questions Т-27, Т-28 and П-17 and the amendments to R11, R21 and R22 spec — about the API contracts, about the tags of `spec`, which now only the release workflow sets, and about the organization's security settings;
  - devcontainer — the base image `devcontainers/base` 3.0.10 by digest; Claude Code is installed without package scripts, its `install.cjs` is run by name, and `claude --version` stops the build on a stub; `git.alwaysSignOff` — a commit from Source Control is signed off by DCO (R22 spec, R30 spec).
- **What is changed here:**

  - the examples of citations of the old template are in inline code, in the five lines where `docs-check-repo` finds them: two in CLAUDE.md, Documents, two in «Ссылках» of RUN.md and one in the opening of the log. Rule 6 of RUN.md and the section Environment in CLAUDE.md name `docs-check-repo`, and in RUN.md — rule 12. These places are as in the template v0.2.9;

— *Why now:* T21 `spec` connects the shared checks from `.github` here (R30 spec), and among them the required "Documents / docs-check-repo". The shared `documents.yml` builds the stage `toolchain` from the `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` of whoever called it and runs `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` in it ([`documents.yml`](https://github.com/altnovatrail/.github/blob/ff2a359/.github/workflows/documents.yml), release v0.1.0 of `.github`, commit ff2a359, read 2026-10-20). And this recipe exists in `spec` only since v0.2.3 (R32 spec): on v0.1.0 the required check would be red and would hold any merge. v0.2.9 is the latest release of `spec` as of 2026-10-20 and immutable (`gh release view -R altnovatrail/spec`); `gh release verify v0.2.9 -R altnovatrail/spec` confirmed it by its attestation, the tag is at commit 5eafb1a. Taking the latest release at the time of the task is what Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec` requires.

— *Rejected:*

- v0.2.6, on which `module-kit` and `tutor` stand (the submodule `spec` on the `main` of each, the GitHub API, 2026-10-20), or another tag older than v0.2.9: Д6 requires the latest release, and rule 12 is in the template only since v0.2.7, R33 spec only since v0.2.8;

— *Consequence:* T07 goes first in table 1 of RUN.md, and T01 depends on it. The commit, push and merge of T07 are done by the executor — on the author's instruction and only in this task («Решено автором» in T07); the following tasks again go by rule 7 of RUN.md. **R02. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.6.3 — the latest release of the line 0.6, the move to which the cross-repository plan puts as the first task of the turn of `learner-model` (R76 spec). The tag closes П-7 and Т-22: the learner model lives in the database of `api`, as an append-only evidence log, and T04 now decides the evidence and the recomputation under one condition — the state appended by new evidence matches the one recomputed from the log (R80 spec). The new item of the card — knowing an anchor skill (R50 spec) — is decided by the new task T09.**
- **What the tag brings to `learner-model`** (`git -C spec diff v0.2.9 v0.6.3`, read 2026-11-02; rows 4.1, 4.3 and 4.6 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`; the parts for `learner-model` in the rows Д12, Д18, Д22, Д30–Д32, Д45, Д53, Д54 and Д67 of table 4 of RUN.md `spec`):
  - the learner model (R80 spec; П-7 and Т-22 closed). It lives in the database of `api`; the append-only evidence log is the source of truth, and the skills' state is a cache with a mark of the version of `learner-model` and the release of `modules`. Evidence is one per finished attempt: what is in it and how much it weighs, `learner-model` decides, and `api` stores it as it is. The condition for `learner-model` is one: "the state appended by new evidence matches the one recomputed from the log", and "if T04 of `learner-model` decides otherwise, Т-22 returns". The engine choice also stores the release of `modules` and the number of the last piece of evidence (architecture 1 spec). How `api` stores all this, R06 and R08 api decided. This is T04 under a new name and section 2.4;
  - the card architecture 3.8 spec — five items "To decide when detailed" instead of three. "Evidence log (Т-22)" became the item "evidence: what is in it and how much it weighs" with the condition of R80 spec, and the new ones are knowing an anchor skill (R50 spec) and lessons (R58 spec). Knowing an anchor skill is T09 and section 2.6. Lessons are an item of `engine`: of it, the learner model takes only that a worked example does not serve as evidence (R58 spec);
  - the skill graph (R49 spec). Assessability is `verdict`, `rubric` or `none`, and a skill `none` is only ever supporting; the tier is computed from the graph, and `module-kit` puts it into the part of the package for the server (R62 spec). The transfer after a major version of a module is by `retired_skills`: the evidence of a retired skill goes to each of its replacements, and the weight is decided by `learner-model`; `[]` — nowhere. The table is closed: the server carries progress over. `api` decided that the transfer is part of every computation from the log, and it gives the graphs in the types of `learner-model` (R08 api). This is T04;
  - anchor skills (R50 spec; Т-14 closed): the shared taxonomy is a vocabulary in the module format with IDs `core.<name>`; in format 0.2 there are four anchor skills. The model knows an anchor skill by this ID, and whether the learner masters it, it decides by the skills that teach it in the learner's modules. An anchor skill is never a primary skill of a task, but it can be a supporting one (the 2026-10-26 amendment to R50 spec). This is T09;
  - the task (R51, R56 spec): primary and supporting skills, and an item's own skills; the difficulty band and the difficulty from 1 to 5 within the band; the starting point is the band of the learner's grade, and how far apart the bands are and where a learner outside the bands starts, `learner-model` decides; the guessing of a choice is 1/k by the number of options. The check kinds of v0 are `number`, `fraction`, `choice`, `predicate` and `rubric`, and an item's verdict is made up of the verdicts of its answer slots. A module's task has no provenance field: in a module everything is from the author. This is T01 and T03;
  - the module (R46 spec): the full ID — the module, the kind and the local ID, `novatrail/olympiad-math/skill/counting-gaps`, and by it the learner model knows a part of a module; the subject in the manifest is the code `mathematics`, `physics` or `writing`. The concept "subject area" is still not in the vocabulary (concepts 15 spec). This is T01;
  - the first slice (R41 spec; О-1 closed): olympiad mathematics 1–6, school mathematics of grade 3 on the workbook and short essays in English. The two maths are one subject area, and only the shared taxonomy links them; a series of items of one type is a page of the workbook; there are no language modules in the slice. This is T01–T03 and sections 2.1–2.3;
  - the withdrawal of a version (R63 spec): the evidence of attempts on a version withdrawn because of a wrong key, `wrong_key`, no longer goes into the model, and it is recomputed from the log. This is T04;
  - a hint at the learner's request is the open question О-21: how it enters evidence is decided together with the weights of `learner-model`. Format v0 has no sketch "how to start" (R57 spec). The model sees a task template as one task, whichever instance comes up (R60 spec; О-12 closed). This is T03 and T04;
  - the random generator is PCG, and the places of randomness are as in standalone's harness (R59 spec; Т-18 closed). Whether the model needs randomness, `spec` does not decide. This is section 1;
  - the cross-repository plan (R76 spec; Д53 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`) is `COORDINATION.md` at the root of `spec`. The move to a tag of the line 0.6 is the first task of the turn of `learner-model`, row 4.1; the detailing is row 4.3, and the code together with `engine` through the gate of `sim` is row 4.6, after the harness and its first tag (row 4.5). `learner-model` and `engine` take a new format version with a new `sim` by a move of their own, and hold the gate on the previous tag (R44 spec; repositories 4 spec). The plan does not change the dependencies of the tasks in this RUN.md: the coordinator takes the numbers and the dependencies within the repository from here;

Rules 6 and 8 of RUN.md and AGENTS.md on a review in one round (R35 spec) were taken here as early as 2026-10-21, by commit b323261;

Of R62 spec on the module package, only the tier matters here, above. They do not change the documents of `learner-model`.

## The learner model

**R03. The learner's level is per module, and standalone's correction for a topic belongs to the skill: the estimate of mastery of a skill is the module's level plus the skill's correction. The scale of the bands, the starting point, the trial series, the step and the cautious "mastered" are as in standalone, and guessing is by the item's answer slots. The threshold "mastered" lives in `learner-model`.** The author's answers of 2026-11-02 in the planning of T01 (RUN.md), all eleven by the plan's recommendations. The whole model is [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1; the sources are section 4 there.
- **What is decided:**
  - the level θ — each module of the learner has its own, on the scale of the bands of this module. The evidence of different modules does not add up in the level: only an anchor skill links the modules — by a prerequisite and by the field `teaches` (concepts 4, 11 spec; R50 spec). There is no subject area in the model;
  - the correction δ belongs to a skill of a module with the assessability `verdict`, and the estimate of mastery of the skill is θ + δ. The uncertainty of the estimate is v, as in SPEC 2.5 standalone;
  - the scale is standalone's numbers, one for all modules: the band at place p in the `bands` of the manifest stands at 2.5 · (p − 1), and a task of band p with difficulty d at β = 2.5 · (p − 1) + (d − 3). The bands overlap: the two hardest difficulties of a band reach into the two easiest of the next one (SPEC 2.1 standalone). The place of the band is taken in the version of the module on which the attempt went;
  - the starting point is θ₀, the offset of the start band:
    - the start band is the one whose grades name the learner's grade;
    - a grade that no band names — the nearest band by grades, and at an equal distance the lower one;
    - without a grade and in a module without grades — the first band.

    The first piece of evidence of the module pins it, each next one carries the same, and a change of grade does not move it afterwards, as in standalone (О-56 standalone);
  - the trial series is the first five answers in the module: θ is the point of maximum posterior probability under a normal prior distribution with centre θ₀ and spread σ₀ = 2.5, and the δ do not move in the series (SPEC 2.2.1 standalone). Until the series is over, its answers lie in the module's state;
  - the probability of a correct answer of an item is P = c + (1 − c) · σ(θ + δ̄ − β):
    - δ̄ is the mean δ of the item's primary skills, its own or the task's skills; supporting skills do not enter P;
    - c is the product over the item's answer slots: 1/k for `choice` with k options, 0 for `number`, `fraction` and `predicate`. An item is correct only when all its answer slots are correct ([the format's text](../spec/format/README.md), section 15);
  - the step is as in R187 standalone: θ += K_θ · (S − P), δ += K_δ · (S − P), K_θ = max(0.2 / (1 + 0.05 · n), 0.05), K_δ = 0.4 / (1 + 0.05 · m). β is not updated: the task's hardness is set by the author;
  - the model's counters — n and m in the step and in v, the five answers of the series, the five answers before "mastered" and two errors in a row — count the answers of items with a verdict. "The number of pieces of evidence" in the skill's state is still the number of attempts (concepts 11 spec);
  - "mastered" is as in R187 standalone, with a threshold without guessing:
    - it is set after a correct answer without a hint, in a skill where m ≥ 5;
    - it is set at the highest of the bands where the skill is taught, not above the task's band, at which σ(λ − the band's offset) ≥ 0.71875 at the cautious level λ = θ + δ − √v;
    - it does not move down, and two answers "not yet" in a row remove it.

    0.71875 is P = 0.775 for a task with five options, the middle of standalone's corridor. The threshold is a constant of `learner-model`, and `engine` reads "mastered" and the mastery band.
- **The plan's proposal, not the author's answer:** the numbers of the step, σ₀ = 2.5, I = 0.149981 in v and the way to find the maximum in the trial series — by a grid with step 0.05 and bisection. These are standalone's numbers and solver (SPEC 2.2, 2.2.1, 2.5 standalone); there was no separate question about them to the author.
- **What R03 does not decide:**
  - how the weight of evidence enters the step and the counters, how many answers an attempt of many items gives, how the shift is divided among the primary skills, what the supporting skills get, what a probe gives (R299 standalone) and which outcomes are "a correct answer without a hint" — T03;
  - forgetting — T02;
  - the kind of evidence, the transfer and the append — T04;
  - the graph types — T05;
  - anchor skills and skills with `rubric` and `none` — T09.

  What R03 passes to them is [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 2.2–2.6.

— *Why:*
- **The level per module.** Each module has its own scale of bands, and "numbers are the model's business, not the format's" (R51 spec): the format does not compare the hardness of the tasks of different modules. The band "grade 3" of school mathematics and the band "3–4" of olympiad mathematics are not the same hardness, and a level per subject area would add up their answers on one scale. As the starting model, repositories 6 spec names exactly standalone's model with a level per module. The link between the two maths of the first slice is carried by the shared taxonomy (R41, R50 spec), and the level does not need to carry it.
- **The correction belongs to the skill.** Standalone's topics turn into skills of the module of olympiad mathematics (repositories 7 spec), and "mastered" and the state in concepts 11 spec belong to the skill. This is a hierarchical model of adaptive practice: "we just correct the global skill by the concept skill" ([Nižnan et al. 2015](https://files.eric.ed.gov/fulltext/ED560514.pdf), section 2.3).
- **Standalone's numbers.** The module of olympiad mathematics, where a task is a choice among five options, then computes exactly as standalone, and its test vectors are valid (SPEC 2.8 standalone). Its harness measured the rule "mastered": 3.7 % false against 63.5 % for the former rule, and 4.6 answers against 9.9 (R187 standalone). Standalone itself calls the step 2.5 and the spread of the series a guess and a number of the simulation (SPEC 2.1, 2.2.1 standalone) — the harness will check exactly these.
- **The starting point in the log.** The grade lies in the profile (privacy 4 spec), not in the log. A recomputation that read the grade would, after a change of grade, diverge from the appended state — against R80 spec. Each piece of evidence carries the start band, not only the first: `wrong_key` may cut off the first (R63 spec; R08 api). The place of the band instead of its ID — because the recomputation does not open the packages of earlier versions (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R80 spec, came out in v0.6.4; Д3 in table 4 of RUN.md).
- **The first band without a grade.** A trial series with a spread of one band over five answers moves the learner by a band in either direction (SPEC 2.2.1 standalone), and a weak learner starting from the middle one would more often meet tasks beyond their strength.
- **Guessing by answer slots.** The guessing floor is Birnbaum's model: whoever does not know the answer "will guess, and, with probability c_g, will guess correctly" ([Birnbaum 1968](https://faculty.ucmerced.edu/jvevea/classes/290_21/readings/week%209/Birnbaum.pdf), section 17.3). With a choice among k options, "for question with k options the probability of a correct answer becomes" 1/k + (1 − 1/k) · σ ([Pelánek 2016](https://www.fi.muni.cz/~xpelanek/publications/CAE-elo.pdf), section 2.2). An item is correct only when all its answer slots are correct, so guessing it is guessing each one. For a free answer the learner sees no options, and concepts 11 spec calls its guessing "about zero". Zero is exactly this "about zero", until the harness shows otherwise.
- **A threshold without guessing.** "Mastered" means "solves", not "guesses": a threshold on P with guessing would come earlier on a choice between two than on a number. And 0.71875 gives olympiad mathematics exactly standalone's rule.
- **The threshold in `learner-model`.** "Mastered" depends on the history of answers — five answers in the skill, an answer without a hint, two errors in a row — and concepts 11 spec keeps it in the skill's state. A number of `engine` among the inputs of the computation would make the cache depend on what is not in its mark (R80 spec).
- **The answer of an item as the unit.** The probability is computed per item: an item has its own answer slots and skills (concepts 6 spec). And how many answers a workbook page — a series of items of one type — gives is a question of the weights of T03, and T01 does not decide it.

— *Rejected:*
- a level per subject area by the code `subject`: it needs a shared scale of hardness for the tasks of different modules and authors, and with it a term in concepts 15 spec and a field of format 0.3 (R44 spec);
- a level per module, but the start of a new module from the level in a module of the same subject: the same translation of scales;
- the correction at the tier: the tier is computed from the graph and shifts together with it (R49, R62 spec), and "mastered" belongs to the skill anyway. A correction at the tier and at the skill together: standalone's harness did not check such a model;
- an initial estimate by age, by self-assessment and by a short check — in the first slice:
  - age is stored only as a category (privacy 4 spec), and the bands do not name it;
  - self-assessment belongs to language modules, which are not in the slice (R41 spec);
  - a short check is a choice of tasks, a rule of `engine`.

  All three are "for later" in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.1;
- a step of the bands that the module sets — a field of format 0.3; bands without overlap, with step 5;
- without a grade — the middle band;
- the start band only in the first piece of evidence, the band by ID, the starting point by the grade from the profile at each recomputation;
- the threshold as an input from `engine`; "mastered" that `engine` decides;
- the threshold σ ≥ 0.775 — stricter than standalone, and its harness does not carry over to such a threshold; the threshold P ≥ 0.775 with the guessing of the skill's tasks;
- a small constant of guessing for a free answer and a number of its own for `predicate`: there are no data for them;
- the minimum of δ and the product of probabilities over the primary skills: the first jumps from skill to skill, the second drops P with the number of skills at the same difficulty from the author;
- an attempt as one answer for the counters: this would decide the series of T03 in advance;
- an I that depends on the guessing of each answer: the state would have to accumulate sums, and the harness did not check that. The price — on a choice between two, the uncertainty is understated ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.1).

— *Consequence:*
- В3 and В4 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec»: the card architecture 3.8 spec and concepts 11 spec — a level per module and guessing by answer slots; the open part of R23 spec — the threshold "mastered". T01 `engine` will take the threshold from there.
- [architecture.md](architecture.md) — sections 1, 2.1–2.6, 3 and 4; CLAUDE.md and README.md — "the level kept for each module".
- T04 decides how evidence carries the start band, the place of the task's band and the kind and number of options of each answer slot. T05 decides how the graph types carry the module's bands with their grades and the bands where the skill is taught.

— RUN.md, T01; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2, 3.1 and 4.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* the probability of a correct answer of an item and the step are with the skill's retention: P = c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ(θ + δ̄ − β), and the step is w · (S − P) at the retention at the moment of the answer, where w = 1 at R̄ = 1 (R04). The module of olympiad mathematics computes exactly as standalone while R̄ = 1 — in the trial series and for a skill without earlier practice — and standalone's test vectors are valid at Δt = 0. In the skill's state there is also the stability τ. The author's decision in the planning of T02. — R04; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.2.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* the step, the counters and "mastered" are by evidence (R06). The answer of an item is its first verdict in the attempt. One piece of evidence gives one step, from the state before it, and each of its N answers weighs u = 1/(1 + (N − 1)/3); the δ of a primary skill of the item gets 1/J of the step, and m grows by the whole u. n, m, the five answers of the trial series and the five answers before "mastered" are sums of weights, and the trial series is the whole pieces of evidence of the module while n < 5, with a weighted likelihood. "Mastered" is removed by two failures in a row — pieces of evidence where the skill has fewer than half correct — instead of two answers "not yet" in a row, and it is set by a piece of evidence without a failure with a correct answer without help. For a task of one item solved without help, all this is the rules above, and standalone's test vectors are valid as before. The author's decision in the planning of T03. — R06; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.3.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* the module's start band is written into the evidence by the library's rule "band by grade", and the append replaces it with the band from the state if the module already has one, and gives the evidence back with the band it took. The step — both at the append and at the recomputation — takes the band from the state for a module with a state, and the band of the evidence for a module without a state: the rule is one, and two first attempts of one module, started before and after a change of grade, do not set the appended and the recomputed apart. Beside the place of the band — for the task's band and for the start band — the evidence also carries the band's ID, and the recomputation still reads the place. The ID is for a table of correspondence of bands, if one is ever needed: what was rejected above is the ID instead of the place, not together with it. The band's ID is the author's answer in the planning of T04, the start band at the append is the plan's proposal. — R08; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.5.

— *Amendment 2026-11-07:* the first code of the model, T16, builds the step at once in its final form, that of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3, — by the author's decision in the planning of T16:
- one step per piece of evidence, u = 3 / (N + 2), the share 1/J;
- n and m are sums of weights with a tolerance of 1e-9;
- a failure is fewer than half correct.

What is left to T18 is the reading of evidence — outcomes, help, provenance, traps and τ by the share of correct. Rejected: in T16 — R03 without the amendment of R06, on tasks of one item; then the golden vectors of T16 would be rewritten in T18.

Beyond the documents — the execution's proposal, not the author's answer:
- **Operations:** `BandByGrade(grade, bands)`, `Append(state, evidence, graphs)` and `Recompute(log, graphs)`.
  - `Append` gives the state and the evidence with the start band it took: `api` writes both in one transaction (section 3.5).
  - The empty state is the zero `State{}`, because that is what `api` holds while there is no cache. Rejected: a constructor of the empty state and a requirement of kind 1 for the empty one: an extra step for everyone who calls, with no use for the fold.
- **The answer of an item** is the first verdict "correct" or "not yet" of an item all of whose answer slots are of a known kind. So an item with an unfamiliar verdict or check kind is read without an answer, as section 3.5 requires.
- **Evidence without a single answer** changes nothing — neither the module nor the start band: section 3.3 says that it "moves nothing". Rejected: setting up a state for the module with only the start band. Then the band would be pinned by evidence that the model did not read.
- **What `Append` rejects** beyond the checks of the codec — section 3.1, "What stands since T16". This is everything the step relies on: without a check, a wrong value would silently land in a θ that no log would have given. Rejected: silently passing such inputs — the recomputation would then hide an error of `api`.

Standalone's test vectors are SPEC 2.6, 2.7, 2.9 and 2.12 standalone at commit `6b29dd9`, read 2026-11-07 — the numbers of the tables, without code. 2.6 goes from n = 0 by an Elo step, bypassing the trial series, and a test inside the package checks it. — RUN.md, T16; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1.

— *Amendment 2026-11-13:* **n is no longer the number of answers in the module: after a pause of the module of 14 days or more it drops to 20 before the step, and with it the step of θ and the v of each skill of the module widen. This is the forgetting of the module's level, the 2026-11-13 amendment to R04.** In the trial series n < 5, and the rule does not touch it: the five answers of the series and the five answers before "mastered" are the former sums of weights. — RUN.md, T44; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.2.

**R04. Forgetting is the retention of a skill whose stability grows with spaced practice: the probability of a correct answer of an item is multiplied by the retention, which falls by a power law with the time since the skill's last practice. The law and the constants are one for all modules. Forgetting does not remove "mastered" but gives a review date, and `engine` gets the estimate for the time of the choice and this date. The current time does not enter the cache.** The author's answers of 2026-11-02 in the planning of T02 (RUN.md), all five by the plan's recommendations. The model is [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2; the sources are section 4 there.
- **What is decided:**
  - a skill of a module with the assessability `verdict` has the stability τ in days and the time of the last practice t_last. The retention R(Δt, τ) falls with the time Δt since the last practice, and R(0) = 1. A skill without practice is not forgotten: its R = 1. The module's level θ is not forgotten;
  - the probability of a correct answer of an item is P = c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ(θ + δ̄ − β), where R̄ is the retention of the item's primary skills. At R̄ = 1 this is the formula of R03;
  - the stability grows after a correct answer — the more, the lower R was — and falls after "not yet". Within a session R ≈ 1, and τ barely grows: practice in a row does not hold for long;
  - at a piece of evidence R is taken by the time of practice from the log, and "for now" — at reading, by the time that comes from outside. τ and t_last are set by the log, and they go into the cache, while the current time does not;
  - the law and the constants are one for all modules: they are constants of the version of `learner-model`, and they change only through the harness;
  - forgetting does not remove "mastered": answers set and remove it, as in R03. Time gives a review date — the moment when the cautious probability with retention, R · σ(λ − the offset of the mastery band), falls below the threshold 0.71875;
  - `engine`, at reading for the time of the choice, gets the P of an item with retention, for a skill — the estimate and the retention at that time, and for a mastered skill also "mastered" with the mastery band, the cautious probability with retention and the review date, with the mark "already" if the threshold does not hold even without forgetting. Whether it is "due", the engine decides by comparing the date with its own time;
  - the harness checks forgetting on the two maths of the first slice: the virtual learners have a calendar, and their forgetting goes by laws different from the model's law.
- **The proposal of the plan and of the execution, not the author's answer:**
  - the time of practice of a piece of evidence is the time of the attempt's last action, which `api` stores, not the time of its closing (R10 api);
  - R̄ is the mean R of the item's primary skills, like δ̄ (R03);
  - the stability is denoted τ, not S as in FSRS: S in R03 is the outcome of an answer;
  - a power law, as in FSRS-4.5: R = (1 + F · Δt / τ)^(−C), C = 0.5, F = 0.9^(−1/C) − 1 = 19/81. So τ is the pause after which R = 0.9;
  - τ₀ = 1 day — with the skill's first answer;
  - after a correct answer τ := τ · (1 + g · (1 − R)), g = 15: at R = 0.9 the stability grows 2.5 times;
  - after "not yet" τ := max(τ₀, τ / 2);
  - a skill's R in a piece of evidence is one — from t_last to this piece of evidence — and τ changes no more than once per skill per piece of evidence;
  - the step of θ and δ is w · (S − P), w = R̄ · P₁ · (1 − P₁) / (P · (1 − P)), where P₁ is P at R̄ = 1: so the step of R03 carries over into the model with retention, and at R̄ = 1 w = 1;
  - in the trial series P is without retention, R̄ = 1 and w = 1, while τ and the skills' time of practice are written as usual, with R by the time of practice;
  - the date is no earlier than τ₀ after the last practice, and the model computes it at reading, from the cache and without the current time.
- **What R04 does not decide:**
  - which skills a piece of evidence moves — and so whose τ and t_last change —, whether τ grows when not all the skill's items on a page are correct, and whether an attempt with the outcome "solution shown" or "unchecked" is practice — and with the outcome of `api` "left", which is not in `spec` v0.6.3 (R10 api; `R82`) — T03;
  - where the time of practice lies in the evidence, whether the recomputation and the append match bit for bit with the power in the formula of retention, and how τ goes by `retired_skills` — T04;
  - the form of the time, the date and "already" in the types — T05;
  - whether knowing an anchor skill takes retention into account — T09;
  - the repetition rule — when to give a skill with a date, how many repetitions a day, how they share the queue — `engine`.

  What R04 passes to them is [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 2.3–2.6.

— *Why:*
- **Retention with stability.** This is how spaced repetition works: memory fades until practice, and practice grows its strength. In HLR "memory decays exponentially over time", and h is "the half-life or measure of strength in the learner's long-term memory" ([Settles & Meeder 2016](https://aclanthology.org/P16-1174.pdf), section 3.3). In FSRS "The smaller the value of $R$, the larger the $SInc$ value. This means that the spacing effect accumulates over time" ([FSRS, The Algorithm](https://github.com/open-spaced-repetition/awesome-fsrs/wiki/The-Algorithm), FSRS v4). A failed repetition then does not pass for nothing: τ falls by half, the skill's P is low, and it soon comes up for repetition again.
- **Mathematics is forgotten too, and intervals work in it.** By a meta-analysis of 53 articles, "there is substantial skill loss with nonpractice or nonuse", and "cognitive, artificial, and accuracy-based tasks" are lost more than "physical, natural, and speed-based" (Arthur et al. 1998, abstract). In an experiment on mathematics problems, practice in two sessions "had no effect on one-week test scores but virtually doubled four-week test scores", while nine problems per session instead of three "had virtually no effect" ([Rohrer & Taylor 2006](https://files.eric.ed.gov/fulltext/ED505642.pdf), General Discussion). So τ grows from a correct answer after a pause, not from the number of answers in a row.
- **A power law.** "The course of forgetting was best described by a simple power function of time" among six laws (Wixted & Ebbesen 1991, abstract), and in FSRS the law is exponential — 0.9^(t/S) in its notation — in v1 and v3 and a power law since v4. This is how forgetting goes in Ebbinghaus too: "very rapid at the beginning of the process and very slow at the end".
- **The numbers.** C = 0.5 and F = 19/81 are FSRS-4.5's. τ₀ = 1 day is the first interval of SM-2, "I(1):=1", and the first box of the Leitner system, "All cards start out in the 1-day box" ([Settles & Meeder 2016](https://aclanthology.org/P16-1174.pdf), section 3.2). g = 15 — so that at R = 0.9 the stability grows 2.5 times, as the initial E-Factor of SM-2: "With all items associate an E-Factor equal to 2.5". Halving is a step one box down in Leitner's system, where the intervals of the boxes double. There are no data for these numbers — Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md.
- **One for all modules.** There are no data by which a module's author would choose a speed of forgetting, and a field of the format must be known to the engine and the harness before the code (R44 spec). Constants by the subject's code put the subject's code into the core, against the criterion of the first slice (product 10 spec). Language may need something else: forgetting is useful "particularly when we are modeling declarative knowledge (in contrast to procedural skills, which are the typical focus of student modeling)" ([Pelánek 2016](https://www.fi.muni.cz/~xpelanek/publications/CAE-elo.pdf), section 4.3) — this is Д6.
- **"Mastered" is not removed by time.** Then it is a function of the log: the cache, knowing an anchor skill (T09), the engine's prerequisites and the skill map do not change from the calendar alone, without a single answer. The evidence of forgetting is a wrong answer, and two in a row remove "mastered", as in R187 standalone, and it does not move down (SPEC 2.5 standalone). Forgetting shows in P, in the cautious probability with retention and in the date.
- **The date and the estimate together.** T02 `engine` asks what to take as the sign of repetition — a threshold of the estimate or a date (RUN.md `engine`, `main`, read 2026-11-02). The model computes the date by the same threshold as "mastered", and the threshold stays one, in one library, as R03 decided. And P with retention is needed by the corridor: after a long break the first task is within the learner's strength.
- **Time bypasses the cache.** `api` recorded: «Время тоже не перенос: забывание решает T02 `learner-model`. Если что-то из этого войдёт в кэш, вопрос возвращается в В4» (R08 api). The cache is only what the log, the graphs and the version set (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R80 spec, came out in v0.6.4). τ and t_last are set by the times of practice in the log, so the appended matches the recomputed (R80 spec). A repeat of the choice takes the time that the choice stores (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R06 api; В4 in table 3 of RUN.md `api`, «в реестр spec»).
- **The time of the last action.** An abandoned attempt is closed by a job after 7 days (R10 api): by the time of closing, the clock of forgetting would start from the day of the job, not from the day of the work.
- **τ once per piece of evidence.** A series of items of one type "are not ten independent pieces of evidence" (concepts 11 spec), and practice in a row does not hold for long (Rohrer & Taylor 2006, above). The ACT-R model comes to the same: "This mechanism limits long-term benefits from further practice at higher levels of activation and produces the spacing effect" (Pavlik & Anderson 2005, abstract).
- **The weight of the step.** The step of R03, S − P, is the derivative of the log-likelihood of the answer, brought to Elo. Retention multiplies this derivative by w, and w · (S − P) is the same step for the model with retention. A wrong answer after a pause then lowers θ and δ little — forgetting explains it — while a correct one moves them almost as without forgetting. Without w, a correct answer after 60 days at τ = 10 would shift θ and δ 3.6 times more than without forgetting, though the skill was not in doubt, and θ would inflate after a break for the whole module ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, the example).
- **The date no earlier than τ₀.** A repetition on the same day barely grows τ: R ≈ 1. Without a lower bound, a skill mastered just barely, at q = 0.72 and τ = 1, would come up for repetition after 21 minutes, and so again and again in one session.
- **The trial series without retention.** Its answers lie in the module's state without time (R03), and the skills it catches are almost all new: their R = 1 anyway.
- **The harness on mathematics.** There are no language modules in the first slice (R41 spec), and mathematics is forgotten too (Arthur et al. 1998, above). Standalone's harness knew no time: "every tenth answer starts a new day" (`docs/learners.md` standalone, c6cbeb2).

— *Rejected:*
- a correction for time only at reading, with the step of R03 as it was: any answer would reset the clock, a failed repetition would barely move the estimate, and the intervals would not grow — a hundred mastered skills would give about fifteen repetitions a day without end. Pelánek's correction for time is not a law of forgetting but a "temporal increase in memory activation due to (short) time from previous exposure of an item", on facts of geography ([Pelánek 2015](https://www.fi.muni.cz/~xpelanek/publications/memory-geography.pdf), section 2.2);
- growth of the uncertainty v with time, as in Glicko, where RD grows "from the passage of time when not playing" ([The Glicko system](http://www.glicko.net/glicko/glicko.pdf)), and in Math Garden, where the uncertainty grows by 1/30 for each day since the previous answer ([Pelánek 2016](https://www.fi.muni.cz/~xpelanek/publications/CAE-elo.pdf), section 2.3). The estimate θ + δ would not fade, and the corridor would not see forgetting. And the skill's part of v in R03 is no more than 1/(20 · I) ≈ 0.333, so the cautious level would fall at most by √0.333 ≈ 0.58 logits, and at n = 200 and m = 50 by 0.25. A skill mastered with a larger margin would never return for repetition;
- FSRS whole — 17 weights in FSRS-4.5, 21 in FSRS-6 — and HLR, where the weights of the features are learned from logs: the weights are fitted to cards and words, and there are no learners' data for them;
- the activation model of ACT-R (Pavlik & Anderson 2005): it needs the whole history of the skill's practice with times and a fitted rate of decay;
- an exponential law, as in HLR and in FSRS v1 and v3: a power law describes forgetting better (Wixted & Ebbesen 1991);
- forgetting of the module's level θ: it is moved by the answers of all the module's skills, while the time of the last practice belongs to the skill (concepts 11 spec). A fall of the level after a break is a profile of the harness, and it will enter the model if the harness shows a need (Д6);
- a speed of forgetting per module — by a field of the next format version — and by the subject's code: above;
- "mastered for now", at reading, and a mastery band that goes down as forgetting proceeds: "mastered" would change with the calendar, and the band would go down contrary to R03;
- only the estimate for now for `engine`: the threshold "due" would live in the engine, a second one beside the threshold "mastered". Only the date: the corridor would not see forgetting;
- waiting for language modules, with forgetting 0 until them: there would be no repetition of the mastered even in mathematics, and the harness's criterion for it would be written later than the first candidate (R163 standalone);
- R̄ as the product or the minimum of the R of the primary skills: for the same reasons for which R03 rejected them for δ̄;
- the step S − P with P with retention, without the weight w: a correct answer after a pause would inflate θ and δ — above, "The weight of the step". The step of R03 with P without retention: a wrong answer after a pause would lower θ and δ as if there were no forgetting;
- a date without a lower bound: above, "The date no earlier than τ₀";
- τ by each item of the page: practice in a row would grow τ, contrary to Rohrer & Taylor 2006;
- a reset of τ to τ₀ after a wrong answer, as in SM-2, where then "start repetitions for the item from the beginning": one error is not yet forgetting, and only two in a row remove "mastered".

— *Consequence:*
- В5, В6 and В7 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec»: what the model gives `engine` for repetition — for T02 engine; what is needed from `sim` — for T01 and T02 sim; that the retention fades, not the estimate itself, and that the skill's state has a stability — to concepts 11 spec and the card architecture 3.8 spec. There is no row of its own about the time of the choice: this is В4 `api`, and forgetting is the second reason. If `spec` does not accept the time of the choice, the question returns here.
- Д6 in table 4 of RUN.md — the constants of forgetting: there are no data for them, and they will be checked only on mathematics.
- The 2026-11-02 amendment to R03 — the probability and the step with retention.
- [architecture.md](architecture.md) — sections 1, 2, 2.2–2.6, 3, 3.1, 3.2 and 4; CLAUDE.md and README.md — forgetting.

— RUN.md, T02; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2, 3.1, 3.2 and 4.

— *Amendment 2026-11-02:* step 2 is a sum over the answers of the piece of evidence, with the weight of the series (R06). The stability of a skill whose answers in the piece of evidence are not all correct changes by the share of correct S̄: τ := max(τ₀, τ · (1 + g · (1 − R))^S̄ · (1/2)^(1 − S̄)), and at S̄ = 1 and 0 these are the rules above. τ, the time of the last practice and the number of pieces of evidence change for the item's primary skills that have an answer in the piece of evidence — both when the solution is shown and for "left"; the evidence does not touch the supporting skills. The author's decision in the planning of T03. — R06; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.3.

— *Amendment 2026-11-07:* forgetting entered the code by task T17. By the author's decisions in its planning:
- the rule of τ by the share of correct is in T17, not in T18. The step since T16 counts the skill's answers in the piece of evidence together, and at S̄ = 1 and 0 the rule gives exactly the rules above. Rejected: the rules above only for "all correct" and "all not correct", and the mixed case by a temporary rule that T18 would rewrite together with the vectors;
- the retention at t₀, the cautious probability with retention and the review date are functions of T17, and `Read` of T20 calls them. Rejected: all the reading in T20 — the date would leave the task whose decision sets it.

The execution's proposal, not the author's answer:
- **The weight w** is R̄ · P₁ · (1 − σ) / (P · (1 − R̄σ)), with the factor 1 − c cancelled, and without guessing (1 − σ) / (1 − R̄σ). At R̄ = 1, w = 1 and P = P₁ exactly, by a separate branch.
  - Why: the formula above would divide small by small at small σ, and at R̄ = 1 the separate branch keeps the bits of T16 for evidence without a pause.
  - Rejected: computing w by the formula above as it is.
- **Δt** is from whole seconds: the difference is taken without overflow and no more than 2⁵³ s. τ is kept below the largest `float64`, and the date goes into seconds with saturation.
  - Why: rule 5 of R09 and rule 4 on NaN and ∞ — for any state, whatever comes.
- **The date without a power:** R*^(−1/C) at C = 1/2 is 1/R*². The second after rounding up is checked by the retention itself, and while R · q there holds the threshold, the date moves by a second. Why: Δt* goes through three roundings and otherwise could land a second before the threshold.
- **Refusal:** `Append` rejects a skill of the state whose τ is less than τ₀. A skill has a state only when it has had practice, and a τ below τ₀ would give R ≈ 0 and a step out of nothing.
  - Rejected: silently raising τ to τ₀ — the recomputation would then hide the error of whoever gave the state.

Standalone's vectors go without a pause, as R03 requires. — RUN.md, T17; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, "What stands since T17".

— *Amendment 2026-11-10:* **g = 15 and halving τ stay: the first judgement of `sim` rejected the candidates g = 10, the multiplier of τ 0.35 and both together.** All three are better than the base by the estimate, but they fail the constraints "no worse", and the common failure is the catch-up after a break `M3.settle` on F-break (R37 `sim`; the 2026-11-10 amendment to R24). Proposing them again — only with new facts about the catch-up after a break. — RUN.md, T32; Д6.

— *Amendment 2026-11-13:* **the module's level θ is forgotten. If from the greatest t_last of the module's skills to the time of practice of the piece of evidence no less than d₀ = 14 days have passed, then before the step n := min(n, N), N = 20 — the constants `levelPause` and `levelWeight`. So after a pause K_θ is 0.1, if n was greater than 20, and not the floor 0.05 at n > 60, and v is wider for each skill of the module. There is no new field — the module's time of last practice is derived from the t_last of its skills — and the state kind stays 1. The item "Rejected" on the forgetting of θ no longer applies: the harness showed a need (R31).** The candidate `theta` of the second judgement of `sim`, `model-2`, chosen by the exploration T43 — by deciding runs of 2,000 working learners per cell (R32). The rule stands in the code only if the judgement `model-2` — T47 RUN.md `sim` — chooses it, the confirmation on the held-out learners passes, and the bands of `sim` are recorded before the merge (R12, R20; R103 spec); otherwise the pull request is closed, and there is no amendment.
- **In the step** the rule stands after the check "no answers", before the step of the series or Elo. Evidence without answers and evidence that weighs nothing change nothing after a pause too ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5). The rule does not touch the answers — they do not read n — so for evidence with answers everything is as in the variant `th20-14` of the exploration, where the rule stood before this check (R32, "Consequence"). In the trial series n < 5 ≤ N, and the rule changes nothing. A module without skills has no pause. Evidence whose answers have no primary skills in the graph, J = 0, moves θ and narrows n but does not set t_last, so the pause of the next piece of evidence is still counted from the skills, and while only such evidence comes, n holds at 20 (R31). At reading there is no rule: `Read` after a pause gives v by the former n until evidence comes.
- *Why:* the analysis of T42 showed that the delay of the catch-up after a break comes from the step with retention with a θ that is not forgotten, while for the base the step of θ at n > 60 stands at the floor 0.05 (R31). The exploration T43: N = 20 and d₀ = 14 hold all the constraints by the rule of the judgement of `sim`. The estimate is +0.0103 [0.0052, 0.0152], the group `jumps` +0.021, the catch-up `M3.settle` on `F-break` faster by 12.7 answers on olympiad and by 42.6 on school, and the corridor `E1.corridor` and `E2` are not shifted anywhere (R32). The price: `M6.never` on olympiad is worse by 0.0025–0.0036 on five `F-` populations — v is wider, and "mastered" comes later — and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school by 1.26 answers at a tolerance of the judgement of 2.61. The objection of R04 — "the time of the last practice belongs to the skill" — is removed by the module's time being derived from the skills: each piece of evidence that moves a skill sets its t_last. The rule differs from the growth of v with time, which R04 rejected, in that it acts only with evidence: the module's v is wider, but it is still the answers that move the estimate.
- *Rejected:*
  - N 10 and d₀ 7 break `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school; N 40 and d₀ 28 hold everything, but more weakly (R32);
  - the rule together with the numbers of forgetting `forget-set` or with one base of halving τ, 0.35 or 0.4: no set holds all the constraints (R32);
  - a separate step of θ after a pause, by a new field of the state, that would not touch n and v: this is state kind 2 and a recomputation of the cache of `api`, and the exploration holds the price of n anyway (R31);
  - the rule at reading, where `Read` would narrow n by the time of reading: this is the growth of v with time, which R04 rejected above, and the exploration did not measure it;
  - a step without forgetting and w = 1 (R31).
- *Consequence:* the merge of T45 releases the patch `v0.1.7`. `api`, by the mark of the model's version, recomputes the cache, and for learners who returned to a module after a pause of 14 days or more at n > 20, the states change. Standalone's vectors and the earlier golden files do not change: they have no pause of the module of 14 days or more at n > 20 — and the new golden log `break` shows it. The 2026-11-13 amendment to R03 — what n means. — RUN.md, T44; Д6; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 2.2, 3, 3.1, 3.2 and 3.5; `model.go`, `step.go` and the tests.

## The tag of `spec` v0.6.6

**R05. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.6.6 — the latest release. The tag brings R82 spec — the showing of the solution, the hint on request, the outcome "left" and the facts of evidence that the weights of T03 read — and the amendments to R80 spec with the conditions of the transfer that T04 waits for. Д3 and Д4 in table 4 of RUN.md are closed.** The author's answer of 2026-11-02 in the planning of T03 (RUN.md): the move is a task of its own, T10, and T03 right after it, in the same session.
- **What the tag brings to `learner-model`** (`git -C spec diff v0.6.3 v0.6.6`, read 2026-11-02; Д70 and Д73 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`):
  - R82 spec, О-7 and О-21 closed. The solution is shown by a button: to a child — from the fourth wrong answer, to a learner of the category `adult` — from the start of the attempt, and with the adult's setting "ask before showing", on by default, — by the adult's action. The showing closes the attempt with the outcome "solution shown" — "not solved", without a trap of its own. A request before the first wrong answer gives "what is known and what to find" and the task's technique, after it — the words of the current rung once more, and "explain differently" — the same rung in other words. The outcome "left" belongs to an attempt that the learner left or abandoned when there were wrong answers in it. Beyond the outcome, the evidence carries the traps the program found, the items correct to the end, with their verdict, the technique — at a step or on request —, the number of "explain differently" and whether the solution of this task — not the template — had gone to the device before, and the weights of the outcome and of each fact belong to `learner-model`. Concepts 6, 10, 11 and 15 spec, the card architecture 3.8 spec and [the format's text](../spec/format/README.md), section 19, say the same. This is the input of T03;
  - two 2026-11-02 amendments to R80 spec. The first is the boundary of the transfer, the answer to the row `api` В8:
    - a skill of a module that is in neither `skills` nor `retired_skills` of the current version goes nowhere;
    - an anchor skill goes as it is if the vocabulary of some format version in the window of `api` knows it;
    - the skills of a trap — by the same rules;

    - evidence carries everything the recomputation reads, and the recomputation does not open the packages of earlier versions;
