## The exploration of E3

**R24. Three candidates go into `sim`'s first judgement, all in the group `forgets`, without a target: g = 10 — one constant; the base of the division of τ 0.35 instead of 0.5 — one; and their coherent set — two. They were chosen by the exploration of T30 — rough runs of the harness on 15 variants of the numbers Д5–Д7, one at a time, at 100 learners per cell, and then of the four best and the coherent set at 300. The other numbers are rejected with what the exploration showed, and the harness does not measure the guessing of `predicate`.** The author's decisions of 2026-11-10 in planning T30 — 100 learners, then 300 for the five best, and the steps by the plan's table; the choice by the results of the exploration, the executor's proposal (RUN.md, T30).
- **The harness** — `sim` `v0.1.2`, commit e61f90a, the tag the gate stands on (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R20): `modules` v0.1.4 with `olympiad-math` 0.1.1, `engine` `v0.1.0` from `sim`'s `go.mod` (В33), the set `working`, the rule `engine`, all populations. `sim` has no recipe yet for a run of another `learner-model` (T31 `sim`), so the run is put together as the gate's recipe puts it: `go.work` in the session's folder with a clone of `sim` and a copy of `learner-model` at the variant's commit, 20 parts `sim run -learners N -part k/20 -commit github.com/altnovatrail/learner-model=<sha>` and `sim merge`. The base is the code of `main` 4f3cfbb. The variants are a commit each on a branch of the clone in the session's folder; neither they nor the files of the runs go into the repository. The SHA-256 of the base: at 100 learners `700bf41b03673fad…20e`, at 300 `6dc6a481c32d8182…be8`; a repeat of the variant "base 0.35" at 100 learners gave the same file, `b70827557e181bff…b69`, to the byte.
- **The analysis** — `sim judge` of the rough look, "a rough look, which decides nothing": a variant stands in the file of the judgement as a candidate for each of the eight groups of the model's criterion and for each target, and `sim compare` gives the differences by measure. The overall score is read only when the candidate's run reads every number with a path (`judge.go`, `score`), and at 100 learners it is not read for any variant, nor for the base against itself: stage 1 ranks by the score of the groups and by the constraints, and the overall score is at 300.
- **Stage 1**, 100 learners per cell — the score of the groups (↑ and ↓ — the interval is wholly above or below zero) and the number of violated constraints "no worse" and of the floor:

  | Variant | `still` | `learns` | `jumps` | `off-band` | `forgets` | `pages` | Violated |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `bandStep` 2.0 | −0.138↓ | −0.234↓ | — | −0.184↓ | +0.141 | 0 | 91 |
  | `bandStep` 3.0 | −0.156↓ | +0.219↑ | +0.121↑ | −0.003 | −0.543↓ | 0 | 83 |
  | σ₀ 2.0 | −0.001 | +0.015 | −0.058 | −0.217↓ | +0.205 | +0.018↑ | 12 |
  | σ₀ 3.0 | −0.004 | +0.020↑ | −0.007 | +0.060↑ | +0.001 | −0.027↓ | 10 |
  | I 0.12 | +0.002 | −0.001 | −0.010 | 0 | +0.205 | +0.002 | 10 |
  | I 0.202148 | −0.001 | +0.005 | −0.005 | 0 | −0.117 | −0.009 | 9 |
  | τ₀ 0.5 days | −0.019↓ | +0.011 | −0.011 | 0 | +0.121 | −0.016↓ | 26 |
  | τ₀ 2 days | −0.005 | −0.018↓ | +0.012 | 0 | −0.313↓ | −0.007↓ | 44 |
  | g 10 | +0.002 | +0.011↑ | +0.015 | 0 | +0.372 | 0 | 3 |
  | g 22.5 | −0.006 | 0 | +0.017 | 0 | +0.051 | 0 | 10 |
  | base 0.35 | −0.002 | +0.002 | +0.025 | 0 | +0.517↑ | 0 | 2 |
  | base 0.7 | 0 | −0.006 | +0.036 | 0 | +0.099 | 0 | 14 |
  | ρ 1/4 | −0.019 | +0.024↑ | +0.041↑ | +0.020↑ | −0.070 | +0.005 | 5 |
  | ρ 1/2 | +0.009 | −0.029↓ | −0.034 | −0.036↓ | +0.372↑ | −0.001 | 20 |
  | help and repeat of a task 1/2 | −0.022 | −0.086↓ | −0.045 | 0 | +0.289 | −0.061↓ | 39 |

  "—" — the group's score is not read. The groups `mastered` and `anchor` are not read at all at 100 learners. 100 learners is below the edge of `sim`'s rough look, 300–1,000 (3.6 `sim`): stage 1 chooses nothing by itself but only sifts for stage 2 — by the sign of the groups and the number of violations. A variant's run took from 10 to 45 minutes on 20 cores — longer when the host was busy from outside.
- **Stage 2**, 300 learners per cell — five runs, as decision 1 requires, "up to the five best": the four best variants of stage 1 and the coherent set of the two numbers that move `forgets` in the same direction. The best are those that have at least one group improved beyond the noise and no more than ten constraints violated. Not selected: ρ 1/2 — `forgets` +0.372↑, but `learns` and `off-band` ↓ and 20 violations; I 0.202148 — 9 violations, but no group moved beyond the noise, and `forgets` −0.117:

  | Variant | Overall score | `forgets` | Other groups | Violated |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | g 10 and base 0.35 | +0.060 [0.023; 0.103] | +0.457 [0.183; 0.784] | within the noise | 8: the corridor `E1` on six populations of the olympiad one, `M1` on G3-drop, `M10` on F-none of the school one, `M3.settle` on F-break — all barely past the tolerance |
  | g 10 | +0.052 [0.018; 0.093] | +0.396 [0.148; 0.702] | within the noise | 4: `E1` on G2, `M1` on G3-drop, `M5.caught` and `M10` on F-none of the school one — all barely past the tolerance |
  | base 0.35 | +0.039 [0.006; 0.079] | +0.315 [0.060; 0.636] | within the noise | 1: `M10.retention` on F-none of the school one, 0.00245 at the tolerance 0.00202 |
  | σ₀ 3.0 | +0.006 [−0.026; 0.042] | +0.089 | `learns` +0.029↑, `off-band` +0.062↑, `still` −0.015↓, `pages` −0.020↓ | 28 |
  | ρ 1/4 | −0.038 [−0.098; 0.020] | −0.283↓ | `learns` +0.026↑, `jumps` +0.020↑, `off-band` +0.020↑ | 18 and the floor `M4` on G0 of the school one: 0.26 at the floor 0.20 |

  No target — `lag`, `corridor`, `settle` — is reached by any variant; the base is far from them: on G3 the share of those "not settled" is 0.88 for the olympiad one and 0.59 for the school one, at the target 0.25.
- **What is decided:**
  1. three candidates go into the judgement — `g10`: `stabilityGrowth` 10 instead of 15; `halve035`: the base of the division of τ 0.35 instead of 0.5; `forget-set`: both. The group of all of them is `forgets`, there is no target, no new field; constants — 1, 1 and 2. Of those that passed `sim` chooses the simplest (R04 `sim`), so the set wins only if its score is above the single ones' beyond the noise;
  2. rejected: `bandStep` — both ways it breaks the error of the estimate `M1` and the corridor `E1` on olympiad maths — dozens of constraints —, and 3.0 besides worsens `forgets`; σ₀ — 2.0 worsens `off-band`, and 3.0 at 300 learners is no better than the base and violates 28 constraints; I — in neither direction moves groups beyond the noise; τ₀ — worse both ways, and 2 days worsens `forgets` too; g 22.5 and base 0.7 — within the noise; ρ — 1/4 worsens `forgets` and breaks the floor, and 1/2 improves `forgets` at the cost of `learns` and `off-band`; the weight 1/2 of an answer with help — worse `learns` and `pages`. The variant took the weight of a repeat of a task too: `helped` counts as help also the solution of this task that has already gone to the device ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3), so there is no separate variant of a repeat;
  3. the guessing of `predicate` did not go into the exploration: no pinned package has a check `predicate` — `grade-3-math` 0.1.0 and `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 have `choice`, `number`, `fraction` and sheets, `english-essays` 0.1.0 has `choice`, `rubric` and sheets (the packages' `server.json`) —, and the synthetic `S-predicate` runs as a cell "without a graph", whose checks are "neither met nor failed". The number stays in Д5 until a module with `predicate`.

— *Why so:* both chosen numbers are constants of forgetting of R04, taken from systems for flashcards, and the exploration showed that the model catches the forgetting of a skill better when the stability grows more slowly and a wrong answer cuts it more (Д6). Both numbers hardly touch the other groups, and the constraints they violate are on the edge of the tolerance, and the base 0.35 has only one. The set — because both numbers move one group in one direction, and the judgement must see whether they add up.

— *What the exploration does not decide:* a rough look decides nothing (3.6 `sim`). At 300 learners no variant holds all the constraints "no worse", and a deciding run at 4,000 learners may not hold them either: its resolution `4,3·SE` is narrower, and the author's tolerance is the same. The exploration ran on `engine` `v0.1.0`; if `sim` raises `engine` to `v0.1.3` by В33, the judgement will run on a new base, and the directions will probably stay: the edit of `engine` is a lesson for a skill from the map, not forgetting.

— *Rejected:*
- a candidate for each group — for σ₀ 3.0 and ρ 1/4 the best groups are paid for by others and by broken constraints, and the criterion judges the overall score;
- more than three candidates — R22;
- a run at 1,000 learners for the three best — about 1 h 40 min per variant on a free machine and more on a busy one, and it does not change the choice: the intervals of the overall score of the three are already above zero.

— *Consequence:* T31 writes three draft pull requests; the base of the division of τ, which stands in the formula `stabilityAfter`, the candidate makes a named constant. The rows Д5–Д7 in table 4 of RUN.md — what the exploration learned. — RUN.md, T30, T31, Д5–Д7; R04, R22; the 2026-11-09 amendment to R20; В33.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10:* **`sim`'s first judgement of the three candidates ended with an exit: all three are better than the base by the estimate, but none holds all the constraints "no worse", and the base stays — g = 15 and the halving of τ. The draft pull requests #43–45 are closed with a reason, and the numbers stay in Д6.** The judgement is T32 of RUN.md `sim`, R37 `sim`, the report is `sim`'s `judgements/model-1/` (`main` 0364b40, read 2026-11-10): deciding runs at 1,000 working learners per cell — that is what a deciding run weighs since T36 `sim` —, on `engine` and `learner-model` v0.1.4 of the base and on the candidates' commits, 41–44 minutes each.

  | Candidate | Estimate | `forgets` | Failed "no worse" |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `g10` | 0.025 [0.011; 0.037] | 0.195 [0.098; 0.294] | 4: `M1.rms.200` on G3-drop, G8 and S-scale1.5 of the olympiad one and `M3.settle` on F-break |
  | `halve035` | 0.014 [0.002; 0.027] | 0.117 [0.031; 0.213] | 3: on the edge of the tolerance — `E3.late` on F-growth5, 0.04967 at 0.049, and `M1.rms.200` on A-unpractised, 0.03116 at 0.03076 —, and `M3.settle` on F-break — 19.42 at 18.11, 7 % past the tolerance |
  | `forget-set` | 0.033 [0.020; 0.046] | 0.277 [0.184; 0.373] | 8: the same as `g10`'s, `E1.corridor` on G2, G2-fading and S-adult and `M1.rms.200` on F-none of the olympiad one |

  What the judgement showed beyond the exploration:
  - the gain in `forgets` was confirmed, but 1.7–2.7 times smaller than at the rough look: at 300 learners — 0.40, 0.32 and 0.46;
  - the common failure of the three is the catching up after a break, `M3.settle` on F-break: with the new numbers the model catches up with the truth after a break for longer. The exploration saw it only for the set, and for `g10`, of `M1.rms.200` on the olympiad one, only G3-drop, without G8 and S-scale1.5: at 300 learners the resolution of "no worse" is wider, and these failures passed for noise. Why the catching up is slower, the judgement does not read: that is a question of the next round, together with Д5–Д7;
  - there was no confirmation, and `held-out-model-1` is still deferred (R37 `sim`).

  — *Why an exit, not a tolerance:* the tolerances "no worse" were recorded before the first candidate, and they may not be shifted for a candidate (R04 `sim`; R163 standalone). — *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code, tests and documents of `main`: the amendments to R04 and the edits of sections 3.2 and 3.3 lived only in the drafts. RUN.md — T32, Д6, row В35 «в реестр spec» to row 4.9 of `COORDINATION.md` and debt Д17 — the gate on the former tag of `sim`. — RUN.md, T32; R37 `sim`.

## The plan of E4

**R25. Epic E4 is format 0.4 in the model. First the move to the latest tag of `spec` and the gate on the latest tag of `sim`, then the value `time` of `CheckKind` and the field of the leading question in one pull request through the gate and in one release, then the result. The executor takes every task of the epic but T37 through to the merge into `main` himself, and T37 the author reads through before the merge.** The author's answer of 2026-11-11 to В36 — take E4, by the row's recommendation —, and the plan of the unfolding, which the author approved the same day (RUN.md, table 1).
- **What was found in planning** (`gh release list`, clones of `spec` and `sim` on `main`, read 2026-11-11):
  - the latest release of `spec` is v0.14.0, and `main` stands on it. Row 4.10 of `COORDINATION.md` sets the order: a release of `learner-model` with `time` and the field of the leading question, then `sim` reads them and releases a tag, and only then `modules` releases v0.2.1; `api` takes the same release (row 6.5; Д138 and Д173 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`). The tag also brings `learner-model` Д181: `api` puts the `.proto` of the version that computed into the export, so the comments of the `.proto` must explain each field without code, and the release must name the `.proto` file a file of the export;
  - the latest tag of `sim` is v0.1.5, commit `dd0391958105195cb21193db1c2b9fbc71b2fe1e`: `engine` and `learner-model` v0.1.4, `modules` v0.2.0 of format 0.4, where there is neither `time` nor leading questions. So the code of Д14 does not move the gate's numbers. `sim`'s E4 «Стенд за библиотеками» is under way: its T39 will release a tag on `engine` v0.1.5, and T42 waits for the release of Д14 from here (RUN.md `sim`, `main` d001515);
  - the code of Д14 is the value `CHECK_KIND_TIME` in `common.proto`, the field in `evidence.proto` after `technique_on_request`, the types, the codec, the check kind in `knownChecks` (`step.go`), which the step, the trial series and `Readout.Task` call, and the rule `helped`. `time` already has no guessing: only `choice` gives it (`model.go`). Whether to raise `EvidenceKind` to 2 — R08 changes the kind when a field is added — is decided in planning the task of the code.
- **What is decided:**
  1. E4 is the next epic, and the second round of candidates, E5, comes after it;
  2. the tasks are T35 «Переход на `spec` v0.14.0», T36 «Гейт на последнем теге `sim`» (Д17), T37 «`time` и наводящий вопрос — одним релизом» (Д14) and T38 «Итог эпика», in this order. The gate takes the tag of `sim` that is the latest at the time of T36; if `sim` releases a new one before the merge of T37, the gate is raised to it by a repeat of T36 — by its own pull request on the code of `main` —, so that a red gate does not confuse new bands with new code. Д181 goes into T37: it edits the `.proto`, and the schema comes out with a release;
  3. the executor himself commits, opens the pull request, waits for green CI and merges each task of E4 into `main` — a departure from rule 7 of RUN.md and from "Do not commit" of RULES.md, as in T29–T34. Except T37: he takes it to green CI and "Gate" and waits, and merges after the author has read the pull request through — the merge releases an immutable release with the schema that `sim` and `api` take, and `api` puts its `.proto` into the export (Д181), so an edit after the merge would become a new release and a recomputation of the cache in `api`. This is the author's answer to a finding of the plan's review.

— *Why so:* row 4.10 puts this release before `sim` and `modules` v0.2.1, and T42 `sim` and `api` wait for it (Д138). A second round without an answer to why the model takes longer to catch up with the truth after a break would repeat the first (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R24). The move goes first, because row 4.10 and Д181 come with the tag, and the repository moves to a new tag by a task of its own (RULES.md, Across repositories). The gate goes before the code, as T29 in E3: the code must pass the gate on the pin on which `sim` will read `time`.

— *Rejected:*
- E5 first — `sim` and `modules` would wait for the release of format 0.4, and the second round would start without an answer on the catching up after a break;
- the code before the move and the gate — the release would pass the gate on the bands of `sim` v0.1.2 with `engine` v0.1.0, not on the pin where `sim` will read it (Д17);
- the move and the gate as one task — each has its own check and its own risk: the move releases a patch with the same code (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R16), and the gate may turn red through no fault of the code.

— *Consequence:* RUN.md — E4 in table 1 with T35–T38, the row of E4 left table 2, В36 is closed, Д14 and Д17 have «Когда вернуться»; CLAUDE.md and README.md — E4 is under way.

— *Amendment 2026-11-11:* **E4 has a fifth task, T40 «`Readout.Task` быстрее» (Д18), after T37 and before T38, and the executor merges T37 himself too.** The author's answers of 2026-11-11: to В37 — «а», by a task of its own in E4 after T37, by the row's recommendation (R27); and in the execution of T37 — «сам доведёшь изменения до мержа в main», so decision 3 no longer holds T37 for the author's reading. T38 waits for T40 too. — RUN.md, table 1, T37, T38, T40, В37 and Д18.

## The `spec` tag v0.14.0

**R26. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.14.0, the latest release. The tag recorded the rows «в реестр spec» В32 and В33 — in Д138 and Д137 —, and the rows are closed, and beyond them it brings the order of the release of format 0.4 — row 4.10 of `COORDINATION.md` — and the format of the export into which `api` puts this library's `.proto` (Д181). The move changes neither the code nor `.devcontainer/`: the output of the recipe `new-repo` is the same byte for byte, so the merge gives no release.** Task T35 of epic E4 (R25).
- **What the tag brings `learner-model`** (`git -C spec diff v0.12.0 v0.14.0`, read 2026-11-11; tables 4 and 5 of RUN.md `spec`; `COORDINATION.md`):
  - row 4.10 of `COORDINATION.md` and Д173 — `spec`'s answer to the row of `sim` В11: the harness reads format 0.4 before the modules. The order: a release of `learner-model` with `time` of `CheckKind` and the field of the leading question, in one release through the gate of `sim` (R19, R21); then `sim` — the translation of `time` and the leading question in the learner's truth on this release, and a tag; then `modules` — v0.2.1 with them. `api` takes the same release before the packages with `time` (row 6.5). This is T37 (R25);
  - Д138 in a new wording — the answer to В32: the move of `learner-model` to a tag does not close its part, it is open until the release with the field and `time`, and `api` waits for this release. In table 5 of RUN.md `spec`, row В32 is recorded with the answer in Д138, so it leaves here, and that the part is open until the release is held by Д14;
  - Д137 in a new wording — the answer to В33: `sim` `v0.1.3` stood on `engine` and `learner-model` v0.1.4, and the first 100 learners of each cell gave the same bytes as the run of the base of T30, so the paths and bands stayed (R32 `sim`). It tells the gates of the libraries to move to `sim` v0.1.3 — T36 takes the latest tag, v0.1.5, which is newer than it. В33 is recorded in table 5 with the answer in Д137 and leaves;
  - R135 spec, Д179 and Д181 — the format of the export, [the part `export/`](../spec/export/README.md): a ZIP of JSON files, where `api` writes the evidence log and the state as the JSON of their messages `novatrail.learnermodel.v1` by protobuf's mapping to JSON with the field names as in the `.proto`, and beside them, in `api/proto/`, the `common.proto`, `evidence.proto` and `state.proto` of the version of `learner-model` that computed, from its release file, which `release-verify` accepted; the rule of `export.proto` requires them in every export with a profile. Д181 tells `learner-model` to keep the comments of the `.proto` such that the reader of an export understands each field without code, and to name in the release the `.proto` file a file that `api` puts into the export. In the schema's archive the files lie under `learner-model-proto-<version>/proto/novatrail/learnermodel/v1/`, and in the export under `api/proto/novatrail/learnermodel/v1/` (the reading vectors of the part `export/`): `api` strips the top folder and `proto/` and puts the files under `api/proto/`, by the package's path;
  - the 2026-11-10 amendment to R09 spec — the open format of the export is the part `export/`, and what the platform keeps as protobuf bytes goes out as JSON of the same value with its own `.proto` (R135 spec); it names R10 of this log. It has no new rule for the library beyond Д181;
  - the 2026-11-10 amendment to R57 spec and Д187 — the trap `twelve-hours-off` for the kind `time` in the format's example `olympiad-math`: a time after noon written as the clock face shows it — the hours counted from noon anew. The model reads the found traps by ID, whatever the kind of the check (R06; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3), so the trap needs no new code, but the test of T37 with a slot `time` may take it as an example;
  - the rest between the tags — R124–R134, R136 and R137 spec on the legal texts and consents, the age of digital consent, the operator's bucket, the reports of a problem, self-hosting and the platform's operator, the parts `legal/` and `consent-age/` of `spec`'s Go module, the 2026-11-09 and 2026-11-10 amendments to `spec`'s decisions on them, the edits of privacy, product, architecture and concepts on the same — does not concern `learner-model`: the library stores nothing and sees no learner's data but what `api` passes to it (privacy 4 spec). ## Format 0.4 in the model

**R28. Format 0.4 in the model's code: the value `CHECK_KIND_TIME = 5` of `CheckKind` with guessing 0 and the field `leading_question_on_request = 20` of the evidence — help, as a technique on request. With the field came the evidence kind 2: it always writes the field, and in kind 1 the field is "not recorded" and is read as "there was no question". The state kind stays 1, the edit does not change the model's numbers, and the comments of the `.proto` are written for the reader of the export (Д181 RUN.md `spec`). In one pull request through the gate of `sim` and in one release.** The author's answers of 2026-11-11 in planning T37 — the name `leading_question_on_request` and kind 2, both by the plan's recommendations; the rest is the plan, which the author approved (RUN.md, T37).
- **What was found in planning** (the code of `main` beafeaa; `sim` and `engine` v0.1.6, read 2026-11-11):
  - the model would read `time` as an item without an answer in one place — `knownChecks` (`step.go`), where the boundary was `CheckKindPredicate`. It is called by the item's answer, the check of an answer of the trial series in the state and `Readout.Task`, so one edit gives `time` to all three. Guessing is already 0 for everything that is not `choice` (`guess`, `model.go`); the codec writes and reads any value of `CheckKind`; there is no `switch` on it anywhere;
  - an attempt where all items are `time` did not move the module and lost its traps: `move` exits before `countTraps`. Now the traps are counted — for example `twelve-hours-off` of the format's example (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R57 spec);
  - the field 15 `technique_on_request` is always written and required in every kind, and in the logic only `helped` reads it; the new field follows the same pattern, but number 20 is written last, after the traps (19);
  - the edit does not break `sim` and `engine` v0.1.6: their literals of `Evidence`, `Task` and `Slot` are by field names, and the translation of a package by `sim` refuses `time` with its own map until T42 `sim` takes it. `sim` compares the bytes of evidence only between two runs, so the gate's numbers do not move: the packages of `modules` v0.2.0 have neither `time` nor leading questions.
- **What is decided:**
  1. **the value `CHECK_KIND_TIME = 5`**, in Go `CheckKindTime`, is a free answer without guessing (R19). It is read the same in every evidence kind: a new value is already an addition that R08 allows, and nobody wrote kind 1 with `time` — the value was not in the schema;
  2. **the field `leading_question_on_request = 20`**, in Go `Evidence.LeadingQuestionOnRequest`: whether the button "Hint" gave the task's leading question. If it did, every answer of the attempt is with help, through `helped`, as with a technique on request (R21). The name follows the neighbouring `technique_on_request`, the number is the next free one;
  3. **the evidence kind 2**, `EvidenceKind = 2`. The kind changes when a field is added, and in the former kind the field is "not recorded", not "no" (R08; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5): `api`'s log holds records of kind 1, where the question could not be recorded, and they must stay distinguishable from records where it was not given. The writer writes field 20 only in kind 2 and newer, and always, `false` too; the strict reader requires it there and rejects kind 1 with field 20; `EncodeEvidence` and `Append` reject kind 1 where the field is set. So the bytes of kind 1 from the log after reading and writing are the same bytes, and "not recorded" is read as "there was no question" — as the former version read kind 1: before format 0.4 there were no leading questions. The state kind is 1: the state got no new fields;
  4. **the comments of the `.proto` are for the reader of the export** (Д181 RUN.md `spec`; R135 spec): `api` puts the `common.proto`, `evidence.proto` and `state.proto` of the version that computed beside the JSON of the log and the state, in `api/proto/novatrail/learnermodel/v1/`. All comments were checked: the headers name the repository and the export, the fields of help say that an answer with them does not set "mastered", the field of the technique at the step that the model does not read it, the level θ the scale of the bands, the kind that kind 2 was added. That the archive's files are the ones `api` puts into the export is said by the README, "Releases", and by [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7: the release's text is written by the shared `release.yml` of `.github`.
- **Checked** (2026-11-11): `just ci-test` — coverage 98.2 %; `just ci-lint`; `just ci-proto` — `buf breaking` against `v0.1.4` is green, the codec and the schema agree both ways; `just ci-release`. The golden logs `testdata/fold/` of the five former logs — bytes of kind 1, as `v0.1.4` wrote them — did not change by a byte and give the same states, and the new `format04` shows that a page after a leading question moves the skill just as without it, but only the page without the question masters it. `just gate exact` from a clone of `sim` `v0.1.8` in the container on this code is green, all 69 numbers to the bit, 98 seconds.

— *Why so:* format 0.4 is out, `sim` and `modules` wait for this release by row 4.10 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`, and `api` writes the field only with it (Д138, Д173 RUN.md `spec`). Kind 2 keeps the distinction that R08 recorded as a rule, and writing the field only in kind 2 is what keeps the codec's promise: what the reader accepted, the writer gives back in the same bytes.

— *Rejected:*
- kind 1 and the field only when `true` — less code, but "the question was not given" and "`api` did not record it" could no longer be told apart in the log, and R08 requires keeping exactly this distinction;
- field 20 always, in kind 1 too — the bytes of kind 1 from the log after reading and writing would become different, and the codec's promise would break;
- `requested_leading_question`, by `spec`'s contract — there it is a string with the text of the question in the conversation between `app` and `api`, and here it is a fact of the attempt beside `technique_on_request`;
- `CHECK_KIND_TIME` only in kind 2 — a needless condition in the step, and there is nothing to distinguish: kind 1 with this value does not occur.

— *Consequence:* the merge changes the release's `paths` (R16) and releases `v0.1.5`. `api`'s cache is marked with the model's version (R80 spec), so `api`, taking it, will recompute the states, but the edit does not change the numbers of the former logs. `spec`, `sim` and `api` learn of the release by a row «в реестр spec»; Д14 is closed. — R08, R10, R16, R19, R21; the 2026-11-11 amendments to R19 and R21; RUN.md, T37 and Д14; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 2.8, 3.1, 3.3, 3.5 and 3.7; `proto/`, `types.go`, `evidence.go`, `codec.go`, `codec_read.go`, `codec_check.go`, `step.go` and the tests.

**R29. `Readout.Task` is faster with the same P to the bit and the same refusals: an ordinary call copies nothing but the slice of the answer. Tags and items that are already in order are read as they are, the module is a substring of the ID, `transfer` gives the graph's skill without a copy, the shares of the transfer are gathered only by the step, and the retentions of the skills at t₀ and the starting point of a module without a state are computed once by `Read`. The API is the same; the reference is `Task` of version `v0.1.5` in the tests.** The author's answers of 2026-11-11 in the planning of T40 — to speed it up inside, with the same API, and to measure `engine.Choose` too — both by the plan's recommendations; the rest is the plan the author approved (RUN.md, T40).
- **What was found in the planning and the measurement** (the code of `main` 355affc; `engine` `main` d0bb4d5; 2026-11-11):
  - in Д198 of `spec`'s RUN.md, 1.34–1.43 ms is the time of the whole choice, not of a call: a third of it goes to 772 calls — about 0.6 µs per call. That is not the computation but the work around it: the profile of `go test -bench` with `-cpuprofile` gave `Task` 79% of the benchmark's time, and in it a notable share is memory allocation and garbage collection, while `detmath` is about 3.5%. Allocations: the copy with sorting of the tags in `idList` and `chanceOf` — 23%, `[]Skill{s}` in `transfer` — 12%, insertions of retentions into `rs` — 11% and of shares into `shares`, which `Task` does not read, — 11%, the copy of the items — 11%, `strings.SplitN` in `moduleOf` — 11%; and the concatenation `"task "+t.ID` before the check;
  - the package `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 from `engine`'s data: three bands, 19 skills, all with a verdict, 772 tasks of one item and one primary skill each, 32 of them with one supporting skill; answer slots — 569 `number`, 196 `choice` of five, 7 `fraction`; 19 templates. The package's tags in a task are in the module's order, so the path with a copy remains for tags out of order.
- **What was decided:**
  1. **checks without copies.** `idList` sorts a copy only if the tags are out of order (`sortedView`, beside `sortedCopy` in `graph.go`); `Task` checks the tags of the task and the items with the function `checkTaskTags` — which is `checkTaskContent` without the items' places — and checks the places on the items in order, with the same message; it copies and sorts the items only if they are not in place. `checkTaskPoint` concatenates the name only on a refusal. `moduleOf` finds the three `/` with the function `strings.IndexByte` and gives the module as a substring of the ID, with the same refusals as `SplitN`. The order of the checks and their messages are the same;
  2. **transfer without copies.** `transfer` gives the graph's skill as a slice of the graph itself, `g.Skills[i:i+1:i+1]` — it is only read — and `chanceOf` takes the item's skills without a copy if they are in order, and gathers the shares of the transfer into a slice the step gives it, and at reading into none. σ, R̄ and P are still computed by one function for the step and for reading ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7);
  3. **retentions at t₀ are in `Read`.** For each module whose current graph `Read` checked, it gives once the R at t₀ of each skill of the graph with a verdict, by ID, with the step's function `retention` — the R of the skill's state, or 1 without one — and the starting point of a module without a state from its own reading. `Task` only reads them: the transfer gives only skills of the graph with a verdict, so each R is found and none is inserted, and the slice is cut by `slices.Clip`, so that an insertion, were there one, would copy it. After `Read` the `Readout` does not change, and `Task` can be called from many calls at once (section 3.6, rule 7). A candidate of a module that `Read` did not read goes the old path;
  4. **the reference is `Task` of version `v0.1.5`.** `task_reference_internal_test.go` holds its text and the texts of its checks word for word, but for the names and two lines: which modules `Read` checked, the reference learns from a new field, and it takes P from `chanceOf` the way the step calls it — R anew, into an empty list, and with the shares of the transfer: this is the very computation `v0.1.5` did at reading. The reference has no copy of its own of `chanceOf` and `transfer` — SonarCloud took it for duplicated code, 4.5% against a threshold of 3% — so the reference holds `Task`'s checks, its R and the starting point, which `Read` now computes, to `v0.1.5`, and P to the step. `TestTaskIsReference` compares `Task` with it to the bit and a refusal to the word: on each prefix of the golden logs in three readings of `readGolden`, on the random logs of the property and on the benchmark's pool, with each task of the log and its variants — tags and items in another order, a repeated tag and place, a choice of one option, options without a mark, place 0, difficulty 6, an ID without a module, not UTF-8, a module with no entry in the graphs and a module that `Read` did not read. `TestModuleOfIsReference` compares `moduleOf` with the former `SplitN` on IDs of every form. The test goes red if the time of the retentions is moved by a second or a word in a refusal is changed. `TestTaskAllocatesOnce` holds one allocation per call on the benchmark's pool: so it shows if the transfer gives a skill whose R `Read` did not compute — then P is the same, but the slice is copied; `TestTaskConcurrently` calls `Task` of one `Readout` from four goroutines at once, under `-race` in `ci-test`;
  5. **the benchmark** is `BenchmarkReadoutTask` in `bench_test.go`: a synthetic module of the shape of `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 and four states, as in `engine`'s benchmark: a new learner, the trial series, the middle of the way — ten skills — and a learner far along — 19 skills, 15 mastered, the review date of one has come. The whole pool per iteration; `ns/task` is a call.
- **Checked** (2026-11-11, AMD Ryzen AI 9 365, 20 threads, 10 runs each, the mean):
  - `Readout.Task` — 697–755 ns per call before the change and 286–346 after, 2.0–2.6 times faster; allocations — 10–12 per call before and one after, the slice of the answer; memory — 465–561 bytes per call before and 24 after;
  - `engine.Choose` on `main` d0bb4d5 — its `BenchmarkChoose` on `olympiad-math` 0.1.1 with a `replace` to this code: 0.96–1.04 ms per choice before and 0.57–0.69 after, 1.5–1.8 times; allocations — 9,538–11,046 before and 1,750–2,558 after, memory 54–63% less. `engine`'s tests on this code are green, the golden choices the same;
  - `just ci-test` — coverage 99.1%; `just ci-lint`; `just ci-proto`; the golden files `testdata/fold/`, `testdata/read/` and `testdata/wire/` — without changes.
  - the job "Gate" of pull request #56 on `sim` `v0.1.8` — all 69 numbers within their bands and to the bit the same as recorded; the session could not run `just gate exact` in the container. The release is `v0.1.6`, commit 7d796e2, checked by `gh release verify` and `release-verify`.

— *Why so:* `sim` will give the gate back 300 learners, and the deciding run 4,000, when the choice becomes faster (Д198, Д199 of `spec`'s RUN.md), and raising the library's version costs it nothing. A change inside touches neither the API nor `engine`: `sim` and `engine` take `v0.1.6` by raising the version, and neither has anything to wait for.

— *Rejected:*
- a new API for many tasks — `Readout.Tasks`, or a task checked once when the pool is loaded: the gain could be larger, but `engine` would need a task of its own and a release of its own, and `sim` would wait for both;
- a lazy cache of retentions in `Readout`, filled by the first `Task`: `Readout` would change after `Read`, and two calls at once would write to one memory (section 3.6, rule 7);
- dropping `Task`'s checks for the modules `Read` read: `Task`'s refusals are part of its contract (section 3.7), and without copies the checks cost little;
- changing `sortedCopy` so that it gives the slice itself: `NewGraph` puts its result into the graph, and the graph would share memory with the caller's input.

— *Consequence:* the merge changes the release's `paths` (R16) and releases `v0.1.6`. `api`'s cache is marked with the model's version (R80 spec), so `api`, having taken it, will recompute the states with the same numbers. `engine` and `sim` learn of the release by a row «в реестр spec»; Д18 is closed. — R07, R09, R12, R16, R20, R25; RUN.md, T40 and Д18; README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7; `readout.go`, `step.go`, `fold.go`, `codec_check.go`, `graph.go` and the tests.

**R30. Epic E5 is the second round of the model's candidates. First the gate on a tag of `sim` with the sizes after `v0.1.6` and, in parallel, the analysis of why the model with the forgetting numbers of the first round takes longer to catch up with the truth after a break, `M3.settle` of `F-break`; then the exploration at the size of the deciding run, the drafts of the candidates, the judgement `model-2` and the summary. The round does not wait for the pin of `modules` v0.2.1. The executor takes each task of the epic to the merge into `main` itself, except the drafts of the candidates.** The author's instruction of 2026-11-11 — «создай ветку; в ней отдельным коммитом сделай T38, отдельным коммитом — всё, что можешь, в таблице 4, далее отдельным коммитом архивируй текущий эпик и разворачивай задачи из эпика E5» — after the analysis of E5's readiness, which the author asked for the same day. The make-up and the order are by the row of E5 in table 2, as updated by T38 (RUN.md, table 1).
- **What was found** (clones of `sim`, `engine` and `spec` on `main`, 2026-11-11):
  - `sim` judges second rounds with the tasks of its E4, «когда придут кандидаты» — the judgement `model-2`, the confirmation on the untouched `held-out-model-1` (R35, R37 `sim`) — and sees a candidate's second library (its T40). The round waits for no separate epic of `sim`;
  - E5 of `engine`, the second round of the corridor, goes, by the opening paragraph of table 2 of `engine`'s RUN.md, «после того, как `learner-model` сдвинет `M3.lag` или ускорит `Readout.Task`» — the second condition was met by `v0.1.6` (R29) — and the decision of `engine`'s author at the summary of its E4 is «E5 — после `learner-model`». A candidate of the model shifts the engine's base, so which second round comes first is the row В43 «в реестр spec»;
  - `sim`'s sizes — the gate 150 learners, the deciding run 1,500 (the 2026-11-11 amendments to R31 and R33 `sim`) — will be raised by its measurement after `v0.1.6` (Д7 `sim`; В39), and there is no task for it in table 1 of `sim` yet;
  - the pin of `modules` v0.2.1 (T43 `sim`) waits for a release of `modules`, and that one for `module-kit` on format 0.4, which has no epic (rows 3.8 and 3.9 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`);
  - in a Claude Code session the classifier did not let `sim` be run from a clone, and the exploration does not go without it.
- **What was decided:**
  1. the tasks T41–T46: the gate on a new tag of `sim` (T41); the analysis of `M3.settle` of `F-break` (T42) — without dependencies, in parallel with the gate; the exploration (T43) — after both, at the size of the deciding run; the candidates as drafts (T44), the judgement and the release (T45), the summary (T46) — as T31–T33 of E3;
  2. forgetting of θ (Д6) is in the exploration if T42 shows that the delay in catching up comes from θ not forgetting over a break. ## Catching up after a break

**R31. With the forgetting numbers of the first round the model takes longer to catch up with the truth after a break because it believes in forgetting more strongly: an answer after a pause with a low R̄ says less about the level, and the module's θ is not forgotten over a break, so a fall of the level, which the law of retention does not describe, it puts down to forgetting for longer. This is a property of the step with retention, not a divergence of the model's law from the law of the harness's truth. Forgetting cannot be removed from the step: on populations without a fall the model then takes forgetting for a fall of the level. Catching up is shortened by forgetting of θ — a wider step of θ after a pause of the module of 14 days — and it needs no new field of the state. It goes into the exploration of T43 on its own and together with the forgetting numbers, and the numbers that speed forgetting up are not explored without it.** Task T42 of epic E5 (R30, decision 1); the plan the author approved on 2026-11-11 (RUN.md, T42).
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.8`, commit d957fc7: `engine` `v0.1.6`, `modules` v0.2.0, the set `working`, `engine`'s rule, 1,500 learners per graph of each population — that is the size of the deciding run at this tag (the 2026-11-11 amendment to R33 `sim`) — on both maths. The run is assembled as the recipe `decisive`: a `go.work` in the session's folder with a clone of `sim`, a variant of `learner-model`, and `engine` at the commit of `gate.json`, 20 parts `sim run -populations … -learners 1500 -part k/20 -commit …` and `sim merge`; the comparison is `sim compare` against the base, paired by learner, with a 95-percent interval. The base is `learner-model` `v0.1.4` from `sim`'s `go.mod`: `v0.1.6` gives the same bits (R29). The candidates of the first judgement are the commits of the drafts #43–45: `g10` 7f735b2, `halve035` 17e0a42, `forget-set` 75c6a90. The diagnostic variants are commits of the clone in the session's folder; neither they nor the files of the runs go into the repository, and they were never published anywhere, so a repetition assembles them anew by the description below — then the numbers are the same, and the file's header names its own SHA:
  - `w1`, 7847d28 — `forget-set` and w = 1 for each answer in the step — the field `w` in `answers`, `step.go` — and P with R̄;
  - `nostep`, 4b95d26 — `forget-set` and a step without forgetting: in `answers` P₁ instead of P and w = 1, while τ and reading are as they are; `nostep-base`, 2b69d24 — the same on top of the base;
  - `theta`, 235e44d — the base and forgetting of θ: in `move`, right after `at := e.PracticeTime`, outside the trial series and for a module with skills, if from the greatest t_last of the module's skills to the time of practice is no less than 14 days, n := min(n; 20) — K_θ 0.1 instead of the floor 0.05, and a wider v; `theta-fs`, 3c603ab — the same on top of `forget-set`;
  - `law` — the truth of `F-break` in the clone of `sim`, commit 8c0285c on top of `v0.1.8`, with the law of `forget-set`: `a.Growth = 10` for `F-break` in `internal/population/population.go` and the base 0.35 instead of 0.5 in `internal/learner/attempt.go`.

  The SHA-256 of the files: the base on `F-break` — `12441811f9e5913c…7f5`, and a repetition gave the same file to the byte; the base on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-exp`, `F-C1` and `F-growth5` — `9a9618339e07ed35…b79`; `g10` — `7bed24d5d11defa9…8f6`, `halve035` — `9db8026222be4046…ef1`, `forget-set` — `1cc325663d9961e4…595`, `w1` — `197998a6d6f68740…a38`, `nostep` — `015a44bf99827aa0…702` and on the five others `8e708bd37d2ea344…54c`, `nostep-base` — `fa5fe8efc0c7d935…f66` and `9e462fa825b97240…997`, `theta` — `a52a27a3c893f1af…320` and `e38b4b538902d076…4fc`, `theta-fs` — `340d89a87bb7a818…d51` and `70161c8e9570f5ba…aba`; on the truth `law` — the base `5a6563097aa625ae…fcf`, `forget-set` `42a776f9ae9c2877…2f4`. A run of `F-break` took from 3 to 10 minutes on 20 cores, of the five other populations from 16 to 38.
- **The yardstick.** "Not worse" is read by the rule of `sim`'s judgement (`notWorseChecks`, `internal/criterion/judge.go` of `sim`): the worse edge of the 95-percent interval of the paired difference is no farther than the tolerance, and the tolerance is the larger of the author's tolerance (`defs`, `measures.go` of `sim`: for `M3.settle` 1 answer, for `M1.rms.200` 0.01, for `M5.caught` 1) and 4.3 standard errors of the difference. The standard error here is taken from the interval of `sim compare`, its width divided by 3.92, and the checks are those the model's criterion puts on the `F-` populations. This is the judgement's yardstick on the `F-` populations alone, not the judgement: T42 did not run the other populations.
- **What the runs showed** — `F-break`, b − a against the base; `M3.settle` — answers until catching up, `M3.unsettled` — the share of those who never caught up, `M3.lag` — the estimate minus the truth from the 101st answer; the last column is the "not worse" checks that the variant fails by the yardstick above, on `F-break` and, where there was a run, on the five other `F-`:

  | Variant | `M3.settle` olympiad | `M3.unsettled` olympiad | `M3.lag` olympiad | `M3.settle` school | "Not worse" failures |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | base | 173.1 | 0.237 | 0.280 | 338.1 | — |
  | `g10` | +13.5 [7.4; 20.1] | +0.011 [−0.011; 0.032] | +0.021 | 0.0 [−1.8; 2.0] | 1: `M3.settle`, 20.09 at a tolerance of 13.91 |
  | `halve035` | +6.3 [−0.7; 13.0] | +0.022 [0.001; 0.043] | +0.014 | +1.8 [−0.6; 4.4] | 0 |
  | `forget-set` | +22.2 [14.9; 29.5] | +0.009 [−0.015; 0.031] | +0.034 | +1.5 [−0.2; 3.3] | 3: `M3.settle`, 29.51 at 15.97; `M1.rms.200` of olympiad; `M5.caught` of school — on the edge |
  | `w1` | +28.0 [20.9; 35.8] | +0.065 [0.040; 0.088] | +0.060 | −3.7 [−7.0; −0.4] | 5 |
  | `nostep` | −51.0 [−58.2; −44.0] | −0.044 [−0.067; −0.022] | −0.262 | −78.5 [−82.4; −74.8] | 3, and 20 on the five others |
  | `nostep-base` | −55.9 [−63.1; −48.7] | −0.040 [−0.064; −0.017] | −0.266 | −78.0 [−81.9; −74.1] | 2: `M6.never` and `E1.corridor` of olympiad, and 19 on the five others |
  | `theta` | −9.8 [−15.0; −4.6] | −0.028 [−0.045; −0.011] | −0.020 | −42.2 [−44.5; −39.8] | 0, and 1 on the five others: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 2.77 at 2.73 |
  | `theta-fs` | +10.0 [3.4; 16.8] | +0.005 [−0.019; 0.028] | +0.016 | −40.4 [−43.2; −37.6] | 1: `M3.settle`, 16.8 at 14.71; and 2 on the five others |

  On the truth `law`, which forgets by the law of `forget-set`, the base catches up in 162.1 answers, and `forget-set` 14.9 [8.2; 21.6] longer; on school maths 3.7 [1.1; 6.4] longer. The first judgement failed `g10` and `halve035` on `F-break` at 1,000 learners, on `engine` `v0.1.4` (R37 `sim`); here `halve035` holds the check: the harness is different — `engine` `v0.1.6` and the packages v0.2.0 — and that is for the judgement to decide, not the analysis.
- **Why so.** The step of θ is K_θ · w · (S − P), where P = c + (1 − c) · R̄ · σ ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2). The lower R̄, the less P depends on θ — the factor R̄ at σ — and the smaller w, so an answer after a pause moves θ toward the truth the more weakly, the more strongly the model believes in forgetting. After the break of `F-break` the truth's level falls by 1.0, while the module's θ is not forgotten: the model sees the fall only as wrong answers and, while R̄ is low, explains them by forgetting. The numbers of the first round grow τ more slowly and cut it harder, their R̄ after pauses is lower — and catching up is slower. Their calibration of P after pauses of 30 days and more is better at that: the bias `M2.bias.pause30` of the base is +0.048, of `forget-set` −0.011 — this is the same shift: the fall of the level is explained by forgetting, not by the level. What does not explain it:
  1. the weight w — `w1` catches up even longer: w damps the correct answer after a pause too, which with a low P would pull θ up;
  2. a divergence of the model's law from the law of the truth: the truth of `F-break` forgets by the law of the base — g = 15 and halving (`internal/learner/attempt.go` of `sim`) — and this would give the candidate an extra delay, but on the truth `law`, with the law of `forget-set` itself, the delay is 14.9 answers of 22.2: most of it comes from the numbers themselves. So there is no row «в реестр spec» for `sim`: `F-break` judges what it should.

  And the long catching up of the base itself — 173 answers, and a quarter of the learners of olympiad maths do not catch up — comes from θ not being forgotten and from the step of θ at n > 60 standing at the floor 0.05: `theta`, with a wider step after a pause, shortens it. So the condition of R30, decision 2, is met: the delay comes from θ not forgetting over a break, which the step with retention after a pause hardly moves — and forgetting of θ goes into the exploration.
- **A step without forgetting is rejected.** `nostep-base` catches up on `F-break` 56 answers faster, but on populations without a fall of the level it errs the other way — it takes forgetting for a fall of the level, and θ goes below the truth: on olympiad maths `M3.lag` on `F-model` is −0.27 instead of −0.02, and `M1.rms.200` is worse by 0.14 on `F-model`, 0.19 on `F-exp`, 0.29 on `F-C1` and 0.23 on `F-growth5`, and by 0.02 on `F-C1` of school maths. On `F-none` of olympiad maths and on `F-model` and `F-none` of school maths its error is, on the contrary, smaller: there the truth does not forget, or forgets more slowly than the model believes. By the judgement's yardstick — 19 failures on the five other `F-` and 2 on `F-break`.
- **Forgetting of θ.** `theta` costs little on the five other `F-`, but not nothing: `M6.never` on olympiad maths is worse by 0.002–0.003 where it is outside the noise — on `F-model`, `F-none` and `F-exp` — v is wider, and "mastered" comes later; Brier and `M10.retention` are worse by less than a tenth of the tolerance; and one check fails by a hair — `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, +1.5 [0.3; 2.8] answers at the judgement's tolerance of 2.73. Better are the false "mastered" on `F-none` and `F-C1` of olympiad maths and `M5.caught` on `F-exp` and `F-C1` of school maths. With `forget-set` the rule gives catching up back 12 answers of 22, but `theta-fs` still fails the check `M3.settle`. On the five other `F-`, `theta-fs` keeps the gain of `forget-set` in forgetting — `E3.late` is better by 0.03–0.12 on olympiad maths and by 0.29–0.36 on school maths, except `F-growth5`, where it is within the noise, and `M10.retention` on olympiad maths by 0.001–0.005 — but fails `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths, 0.0204 at 0.014 — `forget-set` had the same failure in the first judgement — and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, 3.65 at 3.41. Worse than the base outside the noise it also has `M10.retention` on school maths — on `F-none` by 0.0014, two thirds of the author's tolerance, on `F-model` and `F-exp` by 0.0004–0.0005 — and `M1.rms.200` on `F-model`, `F-none` and `F-growth5` of school maths, by 0.0001–0.0006.
- **No field is needed.** The time of the module's last practice is the greatest t_last of its skills (`SkillState.LastPractice`, `state.go`): each piece of evidence that moves a skill sets its t_last. The rule, which acts on the first piece of evidence after a pause, reads it, and it lasts through n, which is already in the state. The price is the meaning of n: the "number of answers" becomes smaller after a pause, and with it, for each skill of the module, not only the one that came back, v is wider and the cautious level λ lower. `engine` reads this too — the estimate, the uncertainty and "mastered" through `Readout` — so the exploration measures the corridor `E1.corridor` and the measures `E2` too, not "mastered" and the review date alone: on `F-growth5` of olympiad maths `theta` has already shifted the corridor by 0.001, within the tolerance. A candidate with the rule changes [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.2, and the state kind stays 1. An edge: a piece of evidence whose answers have no primary skills in the graph, J = 0, moves θ but not t_last — then the module's pause is counted from the last practice of the skills, not from it; T44 checks this with a test. A new field — a separate step of θ after a pause that does not touch n and v — is needed only if the exploration shows that the price of n does not hold "not worse"; that is state kind 2 and a recomputation of `api`'s cache (R08; R80 spec), and the author decides it in the planning of T44 (R30, decision 2).
- **What was decided:**
  1. the cause of the delay is the step with retention with a θ that is not forgotten, not the numbers on their own and not the law of the harness's truth: numbers that speed forgetting up always delay catching up, and in T43 they are not explored without forgetting of θ;
  2. forgetting of θ goes into the exploration of T43 — n := min(n; N) after a pause of the module of no less than d₀ days — with two numbers: N, the strength, and d₀, the threshold of the pause. The diagnostics took N = 20 and d₀ = 14 — the threshold of a skill's pause in `sim`'s measures; the exploration looks for N and d₀ for the rule on its own and together with g and the base of the division of τ — in a set with `forget-set` the rule must give catching up back all 22 answers, not 12, and leave no failure of `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths — and measures it on all populations, with `E1.corridor` and `E2`, and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths;
  3. rejected: a step without forgetting — it breaks the error of the estimate on four of the five `F-` without a fall on olympiad maths and on `F-C1` of school maths; w = 1 — it catches up worse; a row «в реестр spec» for `sim` about the law of the truth of `F-break` — the delay comes from the numbers themselves, not from a divergence of the law.

— *Why so:* the judgement found the failure but not its cause (R37 `sim`), and an exploration without the cause would again choose the forgetting numbers that delay catching up. The runs went only on the `F-` populations — the break and the measures of forgetting are there — so the analysis took hours, not a day. The other populations know pauses too — a session a day with probability 5/7, and a skill waits its turn for days — and T42 did not measure the price of the rules on them: the exploration of T43 measures it on all populations.

— *Rejected:*
- small runs on 300 learners, as in the exploration of the first round: 4.3 standard errors of the difference of `M3.settle` at 1,500 learners are 14–16 answers, and at 300 twice as many, wider than the difference itself (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R24);
- tracing the step through the answers after a pause: the runs told the hypotheses apart without it (the plan of T42);
- a field "time of the module's last practice": it is derived from the skills.

— *Consequence:* nothing changes in the code, the tests and the documents of how it is built. RUN.md — T42 and the input of T43, the row Д6; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.2, "Deferred". — R04, R22, R24 and its 2026-11-10 amendment, R30; R35, R37 `sim`; 3.2 and 3.5 `sim`; Д6.

## The exploration of E5

**R32. One candidate goes to the judgement `model-2` — `theta`, forgetting of θ: n := min(n; 20) after a pause of the module of no less than 14 days, without a new field, the group `jumps`, without a target, two constants. It was chosen by the exploration of T43 — deciding runs of the harness at 2,000 working learners per cell, on all populations — the base and nine variants: forgetting of θ with N 10, 20 and 40 at d₀ 14 and with d₀ 7 and 28 at N 20, the same together with the forgetting numbers of the first round, and a weight of 1/2 for an answer with help. The rule with N = 20 and d₀ = 14 holds all the constraints, is better than the base and shortens catching up after a break. With it the forgetting numbers no longer delay catching up, but none of their sets holds all the constraints: `forget-set` still breaks the error of the estimate and the corridor — most of all outside the `F-` populations, where the rule does not act, but on `F-none` too — and the base of the division of τ alone — both 0.35 and 0.4 — fails one check each. The weight 1/2 for an answer with help is rejected.** The author's decisions of 2026-11-12 in the planning of T43 — the make-up: forgetting of θ and one run of the weight of an answer with help, and not to repeat the step of the bands, σ₀, I, ρ and τ₀ (R24); about ten runs with stopping rules — and in the execution: one more run, the rule of θ with the base 0.4, after `thh20-14` failed one check by a hair. The executor then miscounted and called the budget exhausted, while the run was the tenth, within the budget (RUN.md, T43).
- **The harness** is `sim` `v0.1.10`, commit ff921be, the tag the gate stands on (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R20): `learner-model` v0.1.6, `engine` v0.1.6, the packages of `modules` v0.2.5 with `olympiad-math` and `grade-3-math` 0.3.0 — with `time` and leading questions — the set `working`, `engine`'s rule, all populations. A run is `sim`'s recipe `just decisive engine <file>`, and a variant is `CANDIDATE_LEARNER_MODEL` with the folder of the variant's working copy: the recipe keeps the build by `gate.json`, and the file names the commit. The base is a run without a candidate, by `sim`'s `go.mod`: SHA-256 `7db81ec7bdf8c02a…92e` — exactly `base` in `internal/criterion/paths.json` of `sim`, so the exploration's base is the same one on which the judgement reads the paths. The variants are commits on top of `v0.1.6` (7d796e2) in a clone in the session's folder; neither they nor the files of the runs go into the repository, and they were never published anywhere, so a repetition assembles them anew by the description below — then the numbers are the same, and the file's header names its own SHA:
  - the rule of θ is in `move` (`step.go`), right after `at := e.PracticeTime`: outside the trial series and for a module with skills, if `pauseDays` from the greatest `LastPractice` of the module's skills to `at` is no less than `thetaPause`, then `AnswerWeight` := min(`AnswerWeight`; `thetaWeight`); both constants are in `model.go`, after `secondsPerDay`. The same as `theta` of R31, but with d₀ and N as constants. N no less than 5 is required: n < 5 would return the module to the trial series (`inTrial`);
  - `thN-d` is the rule with N and d₀: `th10-14` e47c860, `th20-14` 90b6236, `th40-14` 796d781, `th20-7` c7aaba7, `th20-28` 4268e96;
  - `thfs20-14`, 4cd32e6 — `th20-14` and `forget-set`: `stabilityGrowth` 10 and the base of the division of τ 0.35 in `stabilityAfter`; `thh20-14`, 6e36283 — `th20-14` and the base 0.35 alone; `thh040-20-14`, 011f089 — `th20-14` and the base 0.4;
  - `help05`, d98e1d6 — the weight w of an answer with help (`helped`) multiplied by 1/2 in `answers`, so that its pull in the step of θ and δ is half as large, as the variant of T30 (R24).

  The SHA-256 of the files: `th10-14` — `3103286a394584c8…8e0`, `th20-14` — `356b4969438ce09f…bef`, `th40-14` — `73164447c7dd9367…b5e`, `th20-7` — `77313baeea2d8ad6…df8`, `th20-28` — `fb3651b2c39b706f…3e1`, `thfs20-14` — `329f803c8aeca9fb…3dc`, `thh20-14` — `4994fd294ed902b6…9e9`, `thh040-20-14` — `1ea79fcaa4b28187…623`, `help05` — `1149cd8721bcda8b…801`. A run took from 36 to 41 minutes on 20 cores, and when neighbours shared the host, 50 minutes.
- **The yardstick** is the judgement `sim judge` with the model's criterion by a judgement file in the session's folder: the base and the variants, each with its group and number of constants. At 2,000 learners it reads as a deciding run: "This is a decisive run". But this is the exploration, not the judgement: the judgement `model-2` runs the same working learners with the same seeds, so on a commit with the same code it will repeat their numbers number for number — the file itself differs by the header, which names the commit — and the confirmation on the untouched `held-out-model-1` (R35 `sim`), which the exploration did not touch, guards against fitting to the working learners. The differences by measures are `sim compare` against the base; below, b − a, with a 95-percent interval.
- **What the runs showed** — the estimate and the group's score with an interval, the "not worse" failures by the judgement's rule; `M3.settle` — answers until catching up on `F-break`, for the base 161.1 on olympiad maths and 336.4 on school maths; the last column is `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, where the rule costs most:

  | Variant | Estimate | Group | Failures | `M3.settle` olympiad | `M3.settle` school | `M5.caught` `F-none` school |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `th10-14` | +0.0127 [0.0075; 0.0179] | `jumps` +0.035 | 1: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 3.54 at 2.61 | −20.7 [−25.5; −16.2] | −75.0 [−77.6; −72.4] | +2.29 [1.14; 3.54] |
  | `th20-14` | +0.0103 [0.0052; 0.0152] | `jumps` +0.021 [0.018; 0.023] | 0 | −12.7 [−17.0; −8.3] | −42.6 [−44.7; −40.5] | +1.26 [0.19; 2.35] |
  | `th40-14` | +0.0063 [0.0022; 0.0101] | `jumps` +0.004 | 0 | −3.2 [−7.2; 0.9] | −7.9 [−8.9; −6.7] | +0.49 [−0.33; 1.26] |
  | `th20-7` | +0.0083 [0.0024; 0.0146] | `jumps` +0.021 | 1: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 2.69 at 2.58 | −13.3 [−18.1; −8.7] | −44.3 [−46.5; −42.2] | +1.47 [0.34; 2.69] |
  | `th20-28` | +0.0068 [0.0025; 0.0111] | `jumps` +0.015 | 0 | −9.0 [−12.6; −5.3] | −31.3 [−33.2; −29.3] | +0.45 [−0.49; 1.35] |
  | `thfs20-14` | +0.0361 [0.0269; 0.0461] | `forgets` +0.277 [0.207; 0.352] | 7: `E1.corridor` on G2, G3 and S-adult and `M1.rms.200` on G3-drop, S-scale1.5 and `F-none` of olympiad, `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 3.05 at 3.01 | +4.5 [−1.2; 10.0] | −38.7 [−41.6; −35.6] | +1.63 [0.30; 3.05] |
  | `thh20-14` | +0.0161 [0.0065; 0.0262] | `forgets` +0.119 [0.045; 0.197] | 1: `M1.rms.200` on G8 of olympiad, 0.01044 at 0.01 | −5.4 [−10.7; −0.1] | −41.0 [−43.6; −38.5] | +1.80 [0.22; 3.47] |
  | `thh040-20-14` | +0.0092 [−0.0003; 0.0190] | `forgets` +0.067 [−0.007; 0.141] | 1: `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school, 4.83 at 3.75 | −4.0 [−9.4; 1.3] | −42.5 [−44.9; −40.1] | +3.07 [1.46; 4.83] |
  | `help05` | −0.0297 [−0.0411; −0.0188] | `learns` −0.080 [−0.082; −0.078] | 62: 46 on school, 13 on olympiad, 2 on the pair of maths, 1 on essays | +4.5 [−0.4; 9.1] | +350.0 [343.0; 357.4] | −2.45 [−4.41; −0.59] |

  "Group" is the score of the group the variant named as its own: the rule of θ — `jumps`, the sets with the forgetting numbers — `forgets`, the weight of help — `learns`; for `thfs20-14` the score in the group `jumps` is +0.008 [−0.010; 0.028] — within the noise. The exploration's judgement of the first stage called `th20-14` and `th40-14` equal to the best, and chose `th20-14`.
- **The price of `th20-14`.** The rule moves numbers only on the six `F-` populations and four numbers on `A-fading`: on the other populations a pause of the module of 14 days does not come, and the files coincide with the base number for number. The corridor `E1.corridor` and the loops `E2` are shifted outside the noise nowhere, so `engine`, which reads the estimate, the uncertainty and "mastered" through `Readout`, pays almost no price. Better are the false "mastered" after a break `M4.false.break` on olympiad maths, by 0.005–0.034 on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-exp`, `F-growth5` and `F-break`, `M1.rms.200` on `F-break` of olympiad maths, by 0.012, and `M5.caught` on `F-exp` of school maths, by 0.55 answers. Worse are `M6.never` on olympiad maths, by 0.0025–0.0036 on `F-model`, `F-none`, `F-C1`, `F-growth5` and `F-break`: v is wider, and "mastered" comes later — and `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths, by 1.26 [0.19; 2.35] answers at the judgement's tolerance of 2.61. Brier `M2` and `M10.retention` are shifted by less than 0.001 — Brier by less than half of the author's tolerance, 0.002. This is what R31 found for `theta` on the `F-` populations of `sim` `v0.1.8`, and on `v0.1.10` the check `M5.caught` holds.
- **Why so.**
  1. The strength N: the smaller N, the faster catching up and the dearer `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths — the answers of a skill after its pause of 14 days or more until the P of the reference task stays within 0.05 of the truth for five answers in a row (3.5 `sim`). Resetting n makes the step of θ after a pause wider, while on `F-none` the truth does not forget and the level does not fall, so a wide step catches up nothing there and only makes P swing around the truth for longer. N 10 fails this check, N 40 hardly moves catching up, N 20 is between them.
  2. The threshold d₀: 7 days catch up a little faster than 14, but fire on ordinary pauses of skills and fail the same check; 28 days are weaker and no cheaper. The threshold of a skill's pause in `sim`'s measures is the same 14 days (R31).
  3. The forgetting numbers: with the rule of θ, catching up for `forget-set` is within the noise of the base, +4.5 [−1.2; 10.0] answers, whereas without it R31 on `sim` `v0.1.8` saw a delay of 22.2 answers: the delay that R31 explained by the belief in forgetting with a θ that is not forgotten, the rule removes. But the failures of `forget-set` in the first judgement outside the `F-` populations — `E1.corridor` on G2 and S-adult, `M1.rms.200` on G3-drop and S-scale1.5 (R37 `sim`) — remained: the rule of θ does not act there, so they come from the growth of τ at g 10 itself. Remained too the failure that the rule was to remove by R31 — `M1.rms.200` on `F-none` of olympiad maths, 0.01196 at 0.01125 — and with it `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths. The base of the division of τ alone breaks the error of the estimate less: at 0.35 — `M1.rms.200` on G8 of olympiad maths, 0.01044 at a tolerance of 0.01, 4% beyond it; at 0.4 G8 holds, 0.0088, but `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths fails, 4.83 at 3.75. This measure on this cell is noisier than the others — the standard error of the difference from 0.6 to 0.9 answers — and at 0.35 it was +1.80, and at 0.4 +3.07: of the two bases neither gives a set that holds everything.
  4. The weight 1/2 for an answer with help: with the leading questions of the packages v0.2.5, help is each answer of an attempt where the button "Hint" gave a question (R28), and the half weight damps these pieces of evidence in the step of θ and δ; what share of the answers they make up, the exploration did not measure. Worst of all is school maths: `M1.rms.200` on G2 is worse by 0.68, `M3.lag` on G2-half by 0.31, catching up on `F-break` by 350 answers. The direction and the strength are the same as in the exploration of T30 on packages without leading questions: `learns` there −0.086, here −0.080 (R24) — so leading questions do not change the conclusion.
- **What was decided:**
  1. one candidate goes to the judgement `model-2` — `theta`: the rule of θ with N = 20 and d₀ = 14, as the variant `th20-14`, the group `jumps`, no target — the rule does not move the share of those who did not catch up on G3 or the lag on G2-half — two constants, no new field, the state kind stays 1. It is one idea with one set of numbers. The exploration's judgement called `th40-14` equal by the estimate, but of the two chose `th20-14`, and `th40-14` does not shift catching up on `F-break` of olympiad maths outside the noise, while that is the round's target (R30). If the confirmation of `theta` does not pass, the judgement does not try a second candidate on the same held-out learners (R35 `sim`), so `th40-14` as a second candidate would add no choice;
  2. rejected: `th10-14` and `th20-7` — they fail `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths; `th40-14` and `th20-28` — they hold everything, but are weaker than `th20-14`; `thfs20-14` — seven failures outside catching up; `thh20-14` and `thh040-20-14` — one failure each, and the judgement runs the same learners with the same seeds and will repeat it number for number. The rule of the tolerance — the larger of the author's tolerance and 4.3 standard errors of the difference of this candidate — was recorded before the first candidate and does not change for a candidate (R04 `sim`), even though the tolerance itself depends on the noise of its difference: for `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths it is from 2.58 to 3.75; `help05` — 62 failures;
  3. Д5 and ρ, τ₀ of Д6 and Д7 were not explored — the author's decision, R24: there are no new facts about them.

— *Why so:* an exploration at the size of the deciding run and on the same harness as the judgement is the lesson of the first round: the coarse look at 300 learners did not see the failures the judgement found (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R24). The price is that the judgement `model-2` of `theta` is already known on the working learners, and the confirmation on the held-out ones decides.

— *What the exploration does not decide:* the judgement and the confirmation are `sim`'s tasks (T45). On essays the rule shifted no numbers — the files of `th20-14` and of the base coincide there. A set that keeps the gain of `forget-set` in forgetting without failures outside `F-` — a base between 0.4 and 0.5, or a growth of τ that slows down with τ itself ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.2, "Deferred") — is a question for the next round.

— *Rejected:*
- a candidate for forgetting — a set of the rule of θ with the forgetting numbers — despite the failure by a hair: the judgement will repeat it, and the candidate's exit would give nothing but a run of the judgement;
- more runs on the base of the division of τ — 0.45 and closer to the base: the author allotted about ten runs to the exploration, and the tenth went to the base 0.4, while `M5.caught` on `F-none` of school maths is so noisy that the exploration cannot find the boundary between 0.4 and 0.5 without several runs per point;
- an exploration on the `F-` populations alone, then a full run for the finalists: the price of the rule outside `F-` for the finalists would be visible too, but a full run of all the variants cost as many hours, and "not worse" was read on all populations at once.

— *Consequence:* T44 writes the draft pull request `theta` — the rule with N and d₀ as named constants, as the variant `th20-14`, tests and golden files — and compares the run of `just decisive` on its commit with the file of `th20-14`. In the variant the rule stands before the check "no answers" in `move`, so a piece of evidence without answers after a pause narrows n too, while [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.5, requires that such a piece of evidence change nothing. The candidate puts the rule after this check: `answers` does not read n, so the answers of a piece of evidence with answers are the same. If the numbers of the candidate's run do diverge from `th20-14` after all, the judgement reads the candidate's run, and R32 gets an amendment. T44 checks the edge J = 0 of R31 with a test. The rows Д5–Д7 of table 4 of RUN.md — what the exploration learned; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.2, "Deferred". — R04, R22, R24, R31; R35, R37 `sim`; RUN.md, T43, T44, Д5–Д7.

— *Amendment 2026-11-13:* **the judgement `model-2` chose `theta`, and the confirmation on `held-out-model-1` passed: on the working learners it repeated the exploration number for number, and on the held-out ones `theta` holds all the constraints and is better than the base. The chosen one came out by the merge of T45 (the second 2026-11-13 amendment to R04).** The judgement is T47 of `sim`'s RUN.md, R46 `sim`; the report `judgements/model-2/report.md` of `sim` at `v0.1.13`, read 2026-11-13. The deciding runs are 2,000 learners per cell, `sim` after `v0.1.12`, on the candidate's commit a063835:
  - the working learners: the estimate +0.01029 [0.005208; 0.01522], the group `jumps` +0.02052 [0.01775; 0.02325], no "not worse" failures — the same numbers as `th20-14` in the table above. The SHA-256 of the candidate's file is `35051542b9fdaf49…7c19`, the same to the byte as the run of T44 on `sim` `v0.1.10`: the run was repeated on another clone and another tag of `sim`;
  - `held-out-model-1`, seed 20261108: the estimate +0.04215 [0.03279; 0.05219], the group `jumps` +0.02025 [0.01765; 0.02294], no failures. The estimate is four times higher than on the working learners, and the score of its own group the same; the confirmation does not require a repetition of the estimate (R46 `sim`);
  - both sets have the same measures that left the estimate: the path not read or not above zero — `M7.hard` on G1 and S-adult, `M4.false.break` on four `F-` and `M4.false.pages` on three `W-` of school maths — the path within the author's tolerance — three measures of school maths and `M9.hasFalse` on A-unpractised of the pair of maths; the base reads no measures of essays, and on the held-out ones also `M4.false.all` on G0-start0.5 of school maths.

  `sim`'s base, paths and bands are now on the commit of `theta`, and `held-out-model-1` is no longer held out: the next round of the model is confirmed on `held-out-model-2` (R46 `sim`). — RUN.md, T45; Д6.

— *Second amendment 2026-11-13 (R37):* the next round that "What the exploration does not decide" speaks of is epic E7, T51–T55 of RUN.md: the base of the division of τ between 0.4 and 0.5 and a growth of τ that slows down with τ, on the base with `theta`.

## The tag v0.16.3 of `spec`

**R33. `learner-model` stands on `spec` v0.16.3 — the latest release. The tag recorded all six rows «в реестр spec», В38–В43, and they are closed. Beyond them it brings Go 1.27.4 in the stage `toolchain` and the row Д215 in `claudeMdExcludes` — the move takes them — and `spec`'s epic E10, the saving of tokens (R143 spec, R145 spec): new rules of RULES.md, the template with `DEBT.md`, `docs/sources.md` and budgets of documents, and the translation of `docs/` into English. That is row 7.8 of `spec`'s `COORDINATION.md`, a task of its own, not the move: when to take it is a question to the author, В45. The move changes neither code nor schema, and the merge releases the patch `v0.1.7` with the same code and schema — the Dockerfile is in the release's `paths` (the 2026-11-09 amendment to R16).** The author's decision of 2026-11-13 — «перейди на spec последней версии»; a task outside an epic, T47 of RUN.md.
- **What the tag brings `learner-model`** (`git -C spec diff v0.15.0 v0.16.3`, read 2026-11-13; `DEBT.md`, table 5 of RUN.md and `COORDINATION.md` of `spec`; `grep -n '`learner-model`' DEBT.md`):

The line `go` in `go.mod` stays `1.27.3`: R09's promise is the same bits in any version of Go no lower than the one `go.mod` names, and the gate holds this line ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.6);

For each repository all this is one task outside an epic by the shared text of T123 spec with a table of differences, and for `learner-model` it is row 7.8 of `COORDINATION.md`: the kind `go`, no hook, four sections of architecture over 24 KB, E5 under way.
